===== FILE: backend/tests/ingest_fakes.py =====
"""In-memory fakes of the EventStore / Notifier Protocols used by the ingest tests."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

from app.models import EventUpdate, IndexedEvent, Listener, NotificationKind
from app.store.base import UpsertResult


class FakeStore:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.events: dict[str, IndexedEvent] = {}
        self.updates: list[EventUpdate] = []
        self.listeners: dict[str, Listener] = {}
        self.cursors: dict[str, int] = {}

    # events
    def upsert_event(self, ev: IndexedEvent) -> UpsertResult:
        old = self.events.get(ev.uri)
        if old is not None and _same(old, ev):
            return "unchanged"
        self.events[ev.uri] = ev
        return "created" if old is None else "updated"

    def get_event(self, uri: str) -> IndexedEvent | None:
        return self.events.get(uri)

    def delete_event(self, uri: str) -> bool:
        return self.events.pop(uri, None) is not None

    # updates
    def add_update(self, upd: EventUpdate) -> None:
        self.updates.append(upd)

    def list_updates(self, event_uri: str) -> list[EventUpdate]:
        return [u for u in self.updates if u.event_uri == event_uri]

    # listeners
    def upsert_listener(self, listener: Listener) -> None:
        self.listeners[listener.uri] = listener

    def delete_listener(self, uri: str) -> bool:
        return self.listeners.pop(uri, None) is not None

    def listeners_for_did(self, did: str) -> list[Listener]:
        return [li for li in self.listeners.values() if li.author_did == did]

    def listener_dids(self) -> list[str]:
        return sorted({li.author_did for li in self.listeners.values()})

    # cursors
    def get_cursor(self, name: str) -> int | None:
        return self.cursors.get(name)

    def set_cursor(self, name: str, value: int) -> None:
        self.cursors[name] = value


def _same(a: IndexedEvent, b: IndexedEvent) -> bool:
    return (a.record, a.cid, a.author_handle) == (b.record, b.cid, b.author_handle)


class FakeNotifier:
    """Records publish calls; with a store, also records whether the event still existed."""

    def __init__(self, store: FakeStore | None = None) -> None:
        self.store = store
        self.published: list[tuple[str, NotificationKind, str]] = []
        self.event_present: list[bool] = []

    async def publish(self, event_uri: str, kind: NotificationKind, message: str) -> int:
        self.published.append((event_uri, kind, message))
        if self.store is not None:
            self.event_present.append(self.store.get_event(event_uri) is not None)
        return 1


class FakeResolver:
    def __init__(self, handles: dict[str, str]) -> None:
        self.handles = handles
        self.calls: list[str] = []

    async def resolve(self, did: str) -> str | None:
        self.calls.append(did)
        return self.handles.get(did)


def utc(*args: int) -> datetime:
    return datetime(*args, tzinfo=UTC)

===== FILE: backend/tests/test_api_events.py =====
from datetime import timedelta

from app.models import EventUpdate


def test_health_and_config(client):
    assert client.get("/api/health").json() == {"ok": True}
    assert client.get("/api/config").json() == {
        "devMode": True,
        "jetstreamEnabled": False,
        "eventNsid": "app.communitymap.event",
        "listenerNsid": "app.communitymap.listener",
    }


def test_list_events(client, store, make_event):
    store.upsert_event(make_event("a", tags=["food"], group="ACM", name="Pizza"))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("b", tags=["music"], start_h=3, end_h=4))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("old", start_h=-3, end_h=-2))
    body = client.get("/api/events").json()
    assert [e["uri"].rsplit("/", 1)[1] for e in body["events"]] == ["a", "b"]
    assert body["cursor"] is None
    assert body["events"][0]["status"] == "upcoming"
    assert body["events"][0]["authorDid"] == "did:plc:test"


def test_list_events_filters(client, store, make_event, now):
    store.upsert_event(make_event("a", tags=["food"], group="ACM", name="Pizza"))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("b", tags=["music"], start_h=3, end_h=4))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("c", tags=["tech"], start_h=5, end_h=6))

    def names(**params):
        events = client.get("/api/events", params=params).json()["events"]
        return [e["uri"].rsplit("/", 1)[1] for e in events]

    assert names(tag=["food", "music"]) == ["a", "b"]
    assert names(group="ACM") == ["a"]
    assert names(q="PIZ") == ["a"]
    before = (now + timedelta(hours=2)).isoformat()
    assert names(before=before) == ["a"]
    after = (now + timedelta(hours=4.5)).isoformat()
    assert names(after=after) == ["c"]


def test_list_events_pagination(client, store, make_event):
    for i in range(3):
        store.upsert_event(make_event(f"e{i}", start_h=1 + i, end_h=10))
    first = client.get("/api/events", params={"limit": 2}).json()
    assert len(first["events"]) == 2 and first["cursor"]
    second = client.get("/api/events", params={"limit": 2, "cursor": first["cursor"]}).json()
    assert [e["uri"][-2:] for e in second["events"]] == ["e2"]
    assert second["cursor"] is None


def test_list_events_validation(client):
    assert client.get("/api/events", params={"limit": 0}).status_code == 422
    assert client.get("/api/events", params={"limit": 101}).status_code == 422
    assert client.get("/api/events", params={"before": "nope"}).status_code == 422
    assert client.get("/api/events", params={"cursor": "garbage!"}).status_code == 400


def test_get_event(client, store, make_event, now):
    ev = make_event()
    store.upsert_event(ev)
    store.add_update(
        EventUpdate(event_uri=ev.uri, text="Moved", source_uri="at://p", created_at=now)
    )
    body = client.get("/api/event", params={"uri": ev.uri}).json()
    assert body["name"] == "Test Event"
    assert body["updates"] == [
        {
            "text": "Moved",
            "sourceUri": "at://p",
            "createdAt": now.isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z"),
        }
    ]
    assert client.get("/api/event", params={"uri": "at://missing"}).status_code == 404
    assert client.get("/api/event").status_code == 422


def test_tags_and_groups(client, store, make_event):
    store.upsert_event(make_event("a", tags=["food", "free"], group="ACM"))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("b", tags=["free"], group="ACM"))
    assert client.get("/api/tags").json() == [
        {"value": "free", "count": 2},
        {"value": "food", "count": 1},
    ]
    assert client.get("/api/groups").json() == [{"value": "ACM", "count": 2}]

def test_events_sort_tags_bbox_and_etag(client, store, make_event, now):
    older = make_event("a", tags=["food"], start_h=1, end_h=4)
    newer_rec = make_event("b", tags=["music"], start_h=5, end_h=8).record.model_copy(
        update={
            "created_at": now + timedelta(minutes=10),
            "location": {"name": "Downtown", "lat": "32.71", "lng": "-117.16"},
        }
    )
    newer = make_event("b", tags=["music"], start_h=5, end_h=8).model_copy(
        update={"record": newer_rec}
    )
    store.upsert_event(older)
    store.upsert_event(newer)

    # sort=newest
    newest = client.get("/api/events", params={"sort": "newest"}).json()["events"]
    assert [e["uri"].rsplit("/", 1)[1] for e in newest] == ["b", "a"]

    # comma-separated tags alias
    tagged = client.get("/api/events", params={"tags": "food,music"}).json()["events"]
    assert len(tagged) == 2

    # bbox filter + invalid bbox
    in_box = client.get("/api/events", params={"bbox": "-117.26,32.86,-117.22,32.90"}).json()
    assert [e["uri"].rsplit("/", 1)[1] for e in in_box["events"]] == ["a"]
    assert client.get("/api/events", params={"bbox": "1,2,3"}).status_code == 422
    assert client.get("/api/events", params={"bbox": "0,50,10,40"}).status_code == 422

    # ETag + 304 Not Modified + invalidation on write
    r1 = client.get("/api/events")
    etag = r1.headers["etag"]
    assert r1.headers["cache-control"] == "no-cache"
    r2 = client.get("/api/events", headers={"If-None-Match": etag})
    assert r2.status_code == 304

    store.upsert_event(make_event("c", start_h=2, end_h=3))
    r3 = client.get("/api/events", headers={"If-None-Match": etag})
    assert r3.status_code == 200 and r3.headers["etag"] != etag
===== FILE: backend/tests/test_expiry.py =====
import asyncio
from datetime import timedelta

from app.config import Settings
from app.expiry import run_expiry_loop, sweep_once


async def test_sweep_notifies_then_deletes(store, notifier, make_event, now):
    old = make_event("old", name="Taco Tuesday", start_h=-3, end_h=-1)
    live = make_event("live", start_h=-1, end_h=1)
    store.upsert_event(old)
    store.upsert_event(live)
    store.subscribe("subscriber1", old.uri)
    store.subscribe("subscriber1", live.uri)
    queue = notifier.connect("subscriber1")

    assert await sweep_once(store, notifier, now, grace_s=0) == [old.uri]

    [n] = store.list_notifications("subscriber1")
    assert (n.kind, n.event_uri, n.message) == ("expired", old.uri, "Taco Tuesday has ended")
    assert queue.get_nowait() == n
    assert store.get_event(old.uri) is None
    assert store.get_event(live.uri) is not None


async def test_sweep_respects_grace(store, notifier, make_event, now):
    ev = make_event(start_h=-2, end_h=-0.5)
    store.upsert_event(ev)
    assert await sweep_once(store, notifier, now, grace_s=3600) == []
    assert await sweep_once(store, notifier, now + timedelta(hours=1), grace_s=3600) == [ev.uri]


async def test_sweep_many_pages(store, notifier, make_event, now):
    for i in range(130):
        store.upsert_event(make_event(f"e{i:03d}", start_h=-3, end_h=-1))
    assert len(await sweep_once(store, notifier, now, grace_s=0)) == 130


class BoomStore:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0

    def query_events(self, q, now):
        self.calls += 1
        raise RuntimeError("boom")


async def test_loop_survives_exceptions(notifier):
    store = BoomStore()
    settings = Settings(expiry_interval_s=0)
    task = asyncio.create_task(run_expiry_loop(store, notifier, settings))
    for _ in range(20):
        await asyncio.sleep(0)
    task.cancel()
    assert store.calls >= 2

===== FILE: backend/tests/test_ingest_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import AsyncIterator

import httpx
import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.api import dev, imports
from app.config import Settings
from app.ingest.processor import CommitProcessor
from app.models import EVENT_NSID
from tests.ingest_fakes import FakeNotifier, FakeStore

ICS = """BEGIN:VCALENDAR
VERSION:2.0
PRODID:-//test//EN
BEGIN:VEVENT
UID:x@example.edu
SUMMARY:Far Future Fair
DTSTART:20991112T180000Z
DTEND:20991112T200000Z
END:VEVENT
END:VCALENDAR
"""


def feed_handler(req: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
    if req.url.host == "down.example":
        raise httpx.ConnectError("down")
    if req.url.path == "/html":
        return httpx.Response(200, text="<html>hi</html>")
    return httpx.Response(200, text=ICS)


def make_app(dev_mode: bool = True) -> tuple[TestClient, FakeStore]:
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(imports.router)
    app.include_router(dev.router)
    store = FakeStore()
    notifier = FakeNotifier(store)
    app.state.store = store
    app.state.notifier = notifier
    app.state.settings = Settings(dev_mode=dev_mode)
    app.state.processor = CommitProcessor(store, notifier)

    async def mock_http() -> AsyncIterator[httpx.AsyncClient]:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(feed_handler)) as c:
            yield c

    app.dependency_overrides[imports.get_http_client] = mock_http
    app.dependency_overrides[imports.get_url_guard] = lambda: lambda url: "internal" not in url
    return TestClient(app), store


def test_import_ical_ok() -> None:
    client, store = make_app()
    r = client.post("/api/import/ical", json={"url": "https://cal.example.edu/feed.ics"})
    assert r.status_code == 200
    assert r.json() == {"imported": 1}
    assert len(store.events) == 1

===== FILE: backend/tests/test_ingest_handles.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import httpx

from app.ingest.handles import HandleResolver, did_document_url, handle_from_doc


def resolver(
    handler: httpx.MockTransport, clock: list[float] | None = None, **kw: object
) -> HandleResolver:
    t = clock if clock is not None else [0.0]
    return HandleResolver(httpx.AsyncClient(transport=handler), clock=lambda: t[0], **kw)  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def doc(handle: str) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {"id": "x", "alsoKnownAs": [f"at://{handle}"]}


def test_did_document_url() -> None:
    assert did_document_url("did:plc:abc") == "https://plc.directory/did:plc:abc"
    assert did_document_url("did:web:example.com") == "https://example.com/.well-known/did.json"
    assert (
        did_document_url("did:web:localhost%3A8080")
        == "https://localhost:8080/.well-known/did.json"
    )
    assert did_document_url("did:web:ex.com:u:alice") == "https://ex.com/u/alice/did.json"
    assert did_document_url("did:key:z6Mk") is None


def test_handle_from_doc() -> None:
    assert handle_from_doc({"alsoKnownAs": ["https://x", "at://alice.test"]}) == "alice.test"
    assert handle_from_doc({"alsoKnownAs": "at://nope"}) is None
    assert handle_from_doc([]) is None


async def test_resolve_plc_and_web() -> None:
    def handle(req: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        if req.url.host == "plc.directory":
            return httpx.Response(200, json=doc("alice.test"))
        return httpx.Response(200, json=doc("bob.example.com"))

    r = resolver(httpx.MockTransport(handle))
    assert await r.resolve("did:plc:alice") == "alice.test"
    assert await r.resolve("did:web:example.com") == "bob.example.com"


async def test_failures_return_none() -> None:
    def handle(req: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        if "500" in str(req.url):
            return httpx.Response(500)
        if "junk" in str(req.url):
            return httpx.Response(200, text="<html>")
        raise httpx.ConnectTimeout("slow")

    r = resolver(httpx.MockTransport(handle))
    assert await r.resolve("did:plc:500") is None
    assert await r.resolve("did:plc:junk") is None
    assert await r.resolve("did:plc:timeout") is None
    assert await r.resolve("did:key:abc") is None


async def test_cache_ttl_and_lru() -> None:
    calls: list[str] = []

    def handle(req: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        calls.append(req.url.path)
        return httpx.Response(200, json=doc("h" + req.url.path[-1]))

    clock = [0.0]
    r = resolver(httpx.MockTransport(handle), clock, ttl_s=10, max_size=2)
    await r.resolve("did:plc:a")
    await r.resolve("did:plc:a")
    assert len(calls) == 1
    clock[0] = 11
    await r.resolve("did:plc:a")
    assert len(calls) == 2
    await r.resolve("did:plc:b")
    await r.resolve("did:plc:c")  # evicts a (least recently used)
    await r.resolve("did:plc:b")
    assert len(calls) == 4
    await r.resolve("did:plc:a")
    assert len(calls) == 5

===== FILE: backend/tests/test_ingest_ical.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from datetime import timedelta

import httpx
import pytest

from app.config import Settings
from app.ingest.ical import event_uri, import_feed, parse_ics, run_ical_loop
from tests.ingest_fakes import FakeNotifier, FakeStore, utc

FEED = "https://calendar.example.edu/events.ics"
NOW = utc(2026, 11, 11, 12)


def vevent(*lines: str) -> str:
    return "BEGIN:VEVENT\n" + "\n".join(lines) + "\nEND:VEVENT\n"


def calendar(*events: str) -> str:
    return (
        "BEGIN:VCALENDAR\nVERSION:2.0\nPRODID:-//test//EN\n" + "".join(events) + "END:VCALENDAR\n"
    )


TACO = vevent(
    "UID:taco@example.edu",
    "SUMMARY:Taco Night",
    "DESCRIPTION:Free tacos",
    "DTSTART:20261112T180000Z",
    "DTEND:20261112T200000Z",
    "LOCATION:Price Center",
    "GEO:32.88;-117.24",
    "CATEGORIES:Food,Free",
    "URL:https://example.edu/taco",
)


def test_parse_full_event() -> None:
    (ev,) = parse_ics(calendar(TACO), FEED, NOW)
    assert ev.uri == event_uri(FEED, "taco@example.edu")
    assert ev.uri.startswith("ical://") and len(ev.uri.split("/")[-1]) == 16
    assert ev.author_did == "ical:calendar.example.edu"
    assert ev.source == "ical"
    r = ev.record
    assert r.name == "Taco Night" and r.description == "Free tacos"
    assert r.starts_at == utc(2026, 11, 12, 18) and r.ends_at == utc(2026, 11, 12, 20)
    assert r.location.name == "Price Center"
    assert (r.location.lat, r.location.lng) == ("32.88", "-117.24")
    assert r.tags == ["food", "free"]
    assert r.source_url == "https://example.edu/taco"
    assert r.created_at == NOW


def test_missing_dtend_and_all_day_and_tz() -> None:
    text = calendar(
        vevent("UID:a", "SUMMARY:No end", "DTSTART:20261112T180000Z"),
        vevent("UID:b", "SUMMARY:All day", "DTSTART;VALUE=DATE:20261113"),
        vevent(
            "UID:c",
            "SUMMARY:Pacific",
            "DTSTART;TZID=America/Los_Angeles:20261112T100000",
            "DURATION:PT30M",
        ),
    )
    a, b, c = parse_ics(text, FEED, NOW)
    assert a.record.ends_at - a.record.starts_at == timedelta(hours=1)
    assert a.record.location.name == "TBA"
    assert b.record.starts_at == utc(2026, 11, 13)
    assert b.record.ends_at == utc(2026, 11, 14)
    assert c.record.starts_at == utc(2026, 11, 12, 18)
    assert c.record.ends_at == utc(2026, 11, 12, 18, 30)


def test_skips_ended_and_malformed() -> None:
    text = calendar(
        vevent("UID:old", "SUMMARY:Past", "DTSTART:20261101T180000Z", "DTEND:20261101T190000Z"),
        vevent("UID:bad", "SUMMARY:Broken", "DTSTART:garbage"),
        vevent("SUMMARY:No uid", "DTSTART:20261112T180000Z"),
        vevent("UID:noname", "DTSTART:20261112T180000Z"),
        vevent("UID:nostart", "SUMMARY:x"),
        TACO,
    )
    assert [e.record.name for e in parse_ics(text, FEED, NOW)] == ["Taco Night"]


def test_not_a_calendar_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        parse_ics("<html>nope</html>", FEED, NOW)


def mock_client(bodies: list[str], status: int = 200) -> httpx.AsyncClient:
    def handle(req: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        body = bodies.pop(0) if len(bodies) > 1 else bodies[0]
        return httpx.Response(status, text=body)

    return httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handle))


async def test_import_feed_upserts_and_notifies_changes() -> None:
    store = FakeStore()
    notifier = FakeNotifier(store)
    moved = TACO.replace("LOCATION:Price Center", "LOCATION:Sun God Lawn")
    client = mock_client([calendar(TACO), calendar(TACO), calendar(moved)])

    assert await import_feed(FEED, store, notifier, client) == 1
    (uri,) = store.events
    first = store.events[uri]
    assert await import_feed(FEED, store, notifier, client) == 1
    assert store.events[uri] is first  # re-import of identical feed is "unchanged"
    assert notifier.published == []
    await import_feed(FEED, store, notifier, client)
    assert notifier.published == [(uri, "updated", "Location changed: Price Center → Sun God Lawn")]
    assert store.events[uri].indexed_at == first.indexed_at


async def test_import_feed_http_error() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(httpx.HTTPStatusError):
        await import_feed(FEED, FakeStore(), FakeNotifier(), mock_client(["x"], status=404))


async def test_run_ical_loop_survives_errors() -> None:
    calls: list[str] = []

    def handle(req: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        calls.append(str(req.url))
        if "bad" in str(req.url):
            raise httpx.ConnectError("down")
        return httpx.Response(200, text=calendar(TACO))

    sleeps: list[float] = []

    async def sleep(s: float) -> None:
        sleeps.append(s)
        if len(sleeps) == 2:
            raise asyncio.CancelledError

    store = FakeStore()
    settings = Settings(ical_feeds=f"https://bad.example/x.ics, {FEED}", ical_interval_s=7)
    client = httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(handle))
    with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
        await run_ical_loop(store, FakeNotifier(), settings, client=client, sleep=sleep)
    assert len(calls) == 4 and sleeps == [7, 7]
    assert len(store.events) == 1


async def test_run_ical_loop_no_feeds_returns() -> None:
    await run_ical_loop(FakeStore(), FakeNotifier(), Settings(ical_feeds=""))

===== FILE: backend/tests/test_ingest_jetstream.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlsplit

import pytest

from app.ingest.jetstream import (
    Backoff,
    CursorSaver,
    JetstreamClient,
    parse_message,
    resume_cursor,
)
from app.ingest.processor import CommitProcessor
from app.models import EVENT_NSID, LISTENER_NSID, POST_NSID
from tests.ingest_fakes import FakeNotifier, FakeStore

BASE = "wss://js.example/subscribe"
DID = "did:plc:alice"


class FakeConnect:
    """Each call pops a script: a list of raw messages, or an Exception to raise on connect."""

    def __init__(self, scripts: list[list[str] | Exception]) -> None:
        self.scripts = scripts
        self.urls: list[str] = []

    def __call__(self, url: str) -> Any:
        self.urls.append(url)
        script = self.scripts.pop(0) if self.scripts else []

        @asynccontextmanager
        async def cm() -> AsyncIterator[AsyncIterator[str]]:
            if isinstance(script, Exception):
                raise script

            async def gen() -> AsyncIterator[str]:
                for m in script:
                    yield m

            yield gen()

        return cm()


class StopAfter:
    """Fake sleep: records delays, cancels the loop after n sleeps."""

    def __init__(self, n: int) -> None:
        self.n = n
        self.delays: list[float] = []

    async def __call__(self, delay: float) -> None:
        self.delays.append(delay)
        await asyncio.sleep(0)  # yield like a real sleep
        if len(self.delays) >= self.n:
            raise asyncio.CancelledError


def make_client(
    connect: FakeConnect, sleep: StopAfter | None = None
) -> tuple[JetstreamClient, FakeStore]:
    store = FakeStore()
    proc = CommitProcessor(store, FakeNotifier(store))
    client = JetstreamClient(BASE, proc, store, connect=connect, sleep=sleep or StopAfter(1))
    return client, store


def event_msg(rkey: str, time_us: int) -> str:
    record = {
        "name": "Taco Night",
        "startsAt": "2026-11-12T18:00:00Z",
        "endsAt": "2026-11-12T20:00:00Z",
        "location": {"name": "Price Center"},
        "createdAt": "2026-11-11T00:00:00Z",
    }
    commit = {"operation": "create", "collection": EVENT_NSID, "rkey": rkey, "record": record}
    return json.dumps({"did": DID, "time_us": time_us, "kind": "commit", "commit": commit})


def query(url: str) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    return parse_qs(urlsplit(url).query)


# ---- pure helpers -----------------------------------------------------------


def test_build_url() -> None:
    client, _ = make_client(FakeConnect([]))
    url = client.build_url([EVENT_NSID, LISTENER_NSID], ["did:plc:a", "did:plc:b"], 123)
    assert url.startswith(BASE + "?")
    q = query(url)
    assert q["wantedCollections"] == [EVENT_NSID, LISTENER_NSID]
    assert q["wantedDids"] == ["did:plc:a", "did:plc:b"]
    assert q["cursor"] == ["123"]
    assert client.build_url([], [], None) == BASE


def test_parse_message() -> None:
    assert parse_message('{"kind":"commit"}') == {"kind": "commit"}
    assert parse_message(b'{"a":1}') == {"a": 1}
    assert parse_message("not json") is None
    assert parse_message("[1,2]") is None


def test_resume_cursor_rewinds_five_seconds() -> None:
    assert resume_cursor(None) is None
    assert resume_cursor(10_000_000) == 5_000_000
    assert resume_cursor(1) == 0


def test_backoff_doubles_and_caps() -> None:
    b = Backoff()
    assert [b.next() for _ in range(8)] == [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 60, 60]
    b.reset()
    assert b.next() == 1


def test_cursor_saver_every_n_and_interval() -> None:
    store = FakeStore()
    now = [0.0]
    saver = CursorSaver(store, "js", every=3, interval_s=5, clock=lambda: now[0])
    saver.seen(10)
    saver.seen(20)
    assert store.get_cursor("js") is None
    saver.seen(30)
    assert store.get_cursor("js") == 30
    saver.seen(40)
    now[0] = 6.0
    saver.seen(35)  # out-of-order time_us never moves the cursor backwards
    assert store.get_cursor("js") == 40
    saver.seen("bogus")
    saver.flush()
    assert store.get_cursor("js") == 40


# ---- connection behaviour ---------------------------------------------------


async def test_consume_processes_and_saves_cursor() -> None:
    connect = FakeConnect([[event_msg("a", 100), "garbage", event_msg("b", 200)]])
    client, store = make_client(connect)
    n = await client.consume_once("js", [EVENT_NSID], [])
    assert n == 3
    assert len(store.events) == 2
    assert store.get_cursor("js") == 200
    assert "cursor" not in query(connect.urls[0])


async def test_consume_resumes_from_rewound_cursor() -> None:
    connect = FakeConnect([[]])
    client, store = make_client(connect)
    store.set_cursor("js", 20_000_000)
    await client.consume_once("js", [EVENT_NSID], [])
    assert query(connect.urls[0])["cursor"] == ["15000000"]


async def test_processor_errors_do_not_drop_connection() -> None:
    connect = FakeConnect([[event_msg("a", 100), event_msg("b", 200)]])
    client, store = make_client(connect)

    async def boom(msg: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        raise RuntimeError("db down")

    client.processor.handle = boom  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    assert await client.consume_once("js", [EVENT_NSID], []) == 2
    assert store.get_cursor("js") == 200


async def test_run_stream_reconnects_with_backoff() -> None:
    connect = FakeConnect([OSError("refused"), OSError("refused"), [event_msg("a", 1)], []])
    sleep = StopAfter(4)
    client, store = make_client(connect, sleep)
    with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
        await client.run_stream("js", [EVENT_NSID], [])
    # fail, fail, success (resets), empty connection (no reset)
    assert sleep.delays == [1, 2, 1, 2]
    assert len(store.events) == 1


async def test_posts_stream_only_with_listener_dids() -> None:
    connect = FakeConnect([])
    client, store = make_client(connect, StopAfter(10**9))
    task = asyncio.create_task(client.run_forever())
    await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
    urls = list(connect.urls)
    assert urls and all(POST_NSID not in u for u in urls)

    listener = {
        "subject": f"at://{DID}/{EVENT_NSID}/ev1",
        "hashtag": "tacos",
        "createdAt": "2026-11-11T00:00:00Z",
    }
    commit = {"operation": "create", "collection": LISTENER_NSID, "rkey": "l1", "record": listener}
    await client.processor.handle({"did": DID, "kind": "commit", "commit": commit})
    await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
    task.cancel()
    with pytest.raises(asyncio.CancelledError):
        await task

    post_urls = [u for u in connect.urls if POST_NSID in u]
    assert post_urls
    assert all(query(u)["wantedDids"] == [DID] for u in post_urls)
    assert all(query(u)["wantedCollections"] == [POST_NSID] for u in post_urls)

===== FILE: backend/tests/test_ingest_processor.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

import pytest

from app.ingest.processor import CommitProcessor, diff_events, extract_hashtags
from app.models import EVENT_NSID, LISTENER_NSID, POST_NSID, EventRecord
from tests.ingest_fakes import FakeNotifier, FakeResolver, FakeStore, utc

DID = "did:plc:alice"
OTHER = "did:plc:mallory"
NOW = utc(2026, 11, 11, 12)
EV_URI = f"at://{DID}/{EVENT_NSID}/ev1"


def event_record(**over: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    rec: dict[str, Any] = {
        "name": "Taco Night",
        "description": "Free tacos",
        "startsAt": "2026-11-12T18:00:00Z",
        "endsAt": "2026-11-12T20:00:00Z",
        "location": {"name": "Price Center", "lat": "32.88", "lng": "-117.24"},
        "tags": ["food"],
        "createdAt": "2026-11-11T00:00:00Z",
    }
    rec.update(over)
    return rec


def commit(
    collection: str,
    op: str,
    rkey: str,
    record: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    did: str = DID,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    c: dict[str, Any] = {"operation": op, "collection": collection, "rkey": rkey, "cid": "bafy1"}
    if record is not None:
        c["record"] = record
    return {"did": did, "time_us": 1, "kind": "commit", "commit": c}


def listener_record(subject: str = EV_URI, hashtag: str = "#TacoNight") -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"subject": subject, "hashtag": hashtag, "createdAt": "2026-11-11T00:00:00Z"}


@pytest.fixture
def store() -> FakeStore:
    return FakeStore()


@pytest.fixture
def notifier(store: FakeStore) -> FakeNotifier:
    return FakeNotifier(store)


@pytest.fixture
def proc(store: FakeStore, notifier: FakeNotifier) -> CommitProcessor:
    return CommitProcessor(store, notifier, clock=lambda: NOW)


# ---- events ---------------------------------------------------------------


async def test_create_event_indexes_it(proc: CommitProcessor, store: FakeStore) -> None:
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "create", "ev1", event_record()))
    ev = store.get_event(EV_URI)
    assert ev is not None
    assert ev.author_did == DID
    assert ev.source == "atproto"
    assert ev.cid == "bafy1"
    assert ev.record.name == "Taco Night"
    assert ev.indexed_at == NOW and ev.updated_at == NOW


async def test_non_commit_messages_ignored(proc: CommitProcessor, store: FakeStore) -> None:
    await proc.handle({"did": DID, "time_us": 1, "kind": "identity", "identity": {}})
    await proc.handle({"did": DID, "kind": "commit"})  # malformed: no commit body
    assert store.events == {}


async def test_invalid_event_skipped(proc: CommitProcessor, store: FakeStore) -> None:
    bad = event_record(endsAt="2026-11-12T10:00:00Z")  # ends before start
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "create", "ev1", bad))
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "create", "ev2", {"name": ""}))
    assert store.events == {}


async def test_update_preserves_indexed_at_and_notifies_diff(
    store: FakeStore, notifier: FakeNotifier
) -> None:
    times = iter([NOW, utc(2026, 11, 11, 13)])
    proc = CommitProcessor(store, notifier, clock=lambda: next(times))
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "create", "ev1", event_record()))
    moved = event_record(location={"name": "Sun God Lawn"})
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "update", "ev1", moved))
    ev = store.get_event(EV_URI)
    assert ev is not None
    assert ev.indexed_at == NOW
    assert ev.updated_at == utc(2026, 11, 11, 13)
    assert notifier.published == [
        (EV_URI, "updated", "Location changed: Price Center → Sun God Lawn")
    ]


async def test_unchanged_update_does_not_notify(
    proc: CommitProcessor, notifier: FakeNotifier
) -> None:
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "create", "ev1", event_record()))
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "update", "ev1", event_record()))
    assert notifier.published == []


async def test_delete_publishes_before_deleting(
    proc: CommitProcessor, store: FakeStore, notifier: FakeNotifier
) -> None:
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "create", "ev1", event_record()))
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "delete", "ev1"))
    assert store.get_event(EV_URI) is None
    assert notifier.published == [(EV_URI, "deleted", "Taco Night was cancelled")]
    assert notifier.event_present == [True]


async def test_delete_unknown_event_is_noop(proc: CommitProcessor, notifier: FakeNotifier) -> None:
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "delete", "nope"))
    assert notifier.published == []


async def test_handle_resolved_on_create_and_reused(
    store: FakeStore, notifier: FakeNotifier
) -> None:
    resolver = FakeResolver({DID: "alice.test"})
    proc = CommitProcessor(store, notifier, handle_resolver=resolver, clock=lambda: NOW)
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "create", "ev1", event_record()))
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "update", "ev1", event_record(name="Taco Fest")))
    ev = store.get_event(EV_URI)
    assert ev is not None and ev.author_handle == "alice.test"
    assert resolver.calls == [DID]


# ---- diff -----------------------------------------------------------------


def test_diff_events_reports_each_change() -> None:
    old = EventRecord.model_validate(event_record())
    new = EventRecord.model_validate(
        event_record(
            name="Taco Fest",
            description="Free tacos and salsa",
            startsAt="2026-11-12T19:00:00Z",
            endsAt="2026-11-12T21:00:00Z",
            tags=["food", "music"],
            group="Taco Club",
        )
    )
    diffs = diff_events(old, new)
    assert diffs[0] == "Name changed: Taco Night → Taco Fest"
    assert diffs[1].startswith("Time changed: ")
    assert "→" in diffs[1]
    assert "Description updated" in diffs
    assert "Tags changed: food → food, music" in diffs
    assert "Group changed: none → Taco Club" in diffs


def test_diff_events_location_details() -> None:
    old = EventRecord.model_validate(event_record())
    new = EventRecord.model_validate(
        event_record(location={"name": "Price Center", "lat": "32.9", "lng": "-117.2"})
    )
    assert diff_events(old, new) == ["Location details updated"]
    assert diff_events(old, old) == []


# ---- listeners ------------------------------------------------------------


async def test_listener_upserted_for_own_event(proc: CommitProcessor, store: FakeStore) -> None:
    await proc.handle(commit(LISTENER_NSID, "create", "l1", listener_record()))
    (li,) = store.listeners.values()
    assert li.uri == f"at://{DID}/{LISTENER_NSID}/l1"
    assert li.record.hashtag == "taconight"
    assert li.author_did == DID


async def test_listener_for_someone_elses_event_rejected(
    proc: CommitProcessor, store: FakeStore
) -> None:
    await proc.handle(commit(LISTENER_NSID, "create", "l1", listener_record(), did=OTHER))
    other_coll = f"at://{DID}/app.bsky.feed.post/x"
    await proc.handle(commit(LISTENER_NSID, "create", "l2", listener_record(other_coll)))
    await proc.handle(commit(LISTENER_NSID, "create", "l3", {"subject": EV_URI}))
    assert store.listeners == {}


async def test_listener_delete_and_change_callback(
    store: FakeStore, notifier: FakeNotifier
) -> None:
    seen: list[list[str]] = []
    proc = CommitProcessor(store, notifier, on_listeners_changed=seen.append, clock=lambda: NOW)
    await proc.handle(commit(LISTENER_NSID, "create", "l1", listener_record()))
    await proc.handle(commit(LISTENER_NSID, "create", "l2", listener_record(hashtag="tacos")))
    await proc.handle(commit(LISTENER_NSID, "delete", "l1"))
    await proc.handle(commit(LISTENER_NSID, "delete", "l2"))
    assert store.listeners == {}
    assert seen == [[DID], []]  # only fired when the DID set changed


async def test_async_listener_callback_awaited(store: FakeStore, notifier: FakeNotifier) -> None:
    seen: list[list[str]] = []

    async def cb(dids: list[str]) -> None:
        seen.append(dids)

    proc = CommitProcessor(store, notifier, on_listeners_changed=cb)
    await proc.handle(commit(LISTENER_NSID, "create", "l1", listener_record()))
    assert seen == [[DID]]


# ---- posts ----------------------------------------------------------------


def post(text: str, facets: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    rec: dict[str, Any] = {"$type": POST_NSID, "text": text, "createdAt": "2026-11-11T12:00:00Z"}
    if facets is not None:
        rec["facets"] = facets
    return rec


async def _with_listener(proc: CommitProcessor) -> None:
    await proc.handle(commit(EVENT_NSID, "create", "ev1", event_record()))
    await proc.handle(commit(LISTENER_NSID, "create", "l1", listener_record()))


async def test_post_with_hashtag_becomes_announcement(
    proc: CommitProcessor, store: FakeStore, notifier: FakeNotifier
) -> None:
    await _with_listener(proc)
    text = "Moved inside due to rain! #tacoNIGHT"
    await proc.handle(commit(POST_NSID, "create", "p1", post(text)))
    (upd,) = store.list_updates(EV_URI)
    assert upd.text == text
    assert upd.source_uri == f"at://{DID}/{POST_NSID}/p1"
    assert upd.created_at == NOW
    assert notifier.published == [(EV_URI, "announcement", text)]


async def test_post_hashtag_from_facet(proc: CommitProcessor, store: FakeStore) -> None:
    await _with_listener(proc)
    facet = {"features": [{"$type": "app.bsky.richtext.facet#tag", "tag": "TacoNight"}]}
    await proc.handle(commit(POST_NSID, "create", "p1", post("see you there", [facet])))
    assert len(store.list_updates(EV_URI)) == 1


async def test_post_partial_or_missing_tag_ignored(
    proc: CommitProcessor, store: FakeStore, notifier: FakeNotifier
) -> None:
    await _with_listener(proc)
    await proc.handle(commit(POST_NSID, "create", "p1", post("#TacoNightOut is fun")))
    await proc.handle(commit(POST_NSID, "create", "p2", post("taconight without hash")))
    await proc.handle(commit(POST_NSID, "create", "p3", post("#taconight"), did=OTHER))
    await proc.handle(commit(POST_NSID, "delete", "p1"))
    assert store.updates == []
    assert [p for p in notifier.published if p[1] == "announcement"] == []


def test_extract_hashtags() -> None:
    rec = post("Hi #One, #two! mid#no #thr-ee.")
    assert extract_hashtags(rec) == {"one", "two", "thr-ee"}

===== FILE: backend/tests/test_main.py =====
"""Integration test: full app wired by main.create_app, dev commit -> feed -> notification."""

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.config import Settings
from app.main import create_app


def commit(op: str, record: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    c = {"operation": op, "collection": "app.communitymap.event", "rkey": "3kabc", "cid": op}
    if record:
        c["record"] = record
    return {"did": "did:plc:host", "time_us": 1, "kind": "commit", "commit": c}


RECORD = {
    "name": "Taco Night",
    "startsAt": "2099-01-01T18:00:00Z",
    "endsAt": "2099-01-01T20:00:00Z",
    "location": {"name": "Price Center"},
    "tags": ["food"],
    "createdAt": "2098-12-01T00:00:00Z",
}


def test_end_to_end(tmp_path):
    settings = Settings(db_path=str(tmp_path / "t.db"), dev_mode=True, frontend_dir="../frontend")
    with TestClient(create_app(settings, background=False)) as client:
        assert client.get("/api/health").json()["ok"] is True
        assert client.post("/api/dev/records", json=commit("create", RECORD)).status_code == 200

        events = client.get("/api/events", params={"tag": "food"}).json()["events"]
        assert [e["name"] for e in events] == ["Taco Night"]
        uri = events[0]["uri"]

        sub = {"subscriberId": "tester-123", "eventUri": uri}
        assert client.post("/api/subscriptions", json=sub).status_code == 204

        moved = {**RECORD, "location": {"name": "Sun God Lawn"}}
        client.post("/api/dev/records", json=commit("update", moved))
        notes = client.get("/api/notifications", params={"subscriberId": "tester-123"}).json()
        assert notes[0]["kind"] == "updated"
        assert "Sun God Lawn" in notes[0]["message"]

        assert client.get("/").status_code == 200  # frontend served


def test_dev_route_disabled_by_default(tmp_path):
    settings = Settings(db_path=str(tmp_path / "t.db"))
    with TestClient(create_app(settings, background=False)) as client:
        assert client.post("/api/dev/records", json=commit("create", RECORD)).status_code == 404

===== FILE: backend/tests/test_netguard.py =====
import socket

from app.ingest.netguard import is_public_url


def fake_resolver(ip: str):
    def resolve(host, port, proto=0):
        return [(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM, proto, "", (ip, port))]

    return resolve


def test_public_host_allowed():
    assert is_public_url("https://calendar.ucsd.edu/x.ics", fake_resolver("132.239.180.101"))


def test_private_and_loopback_hosts_rejected():
    for ip in ("127.0.0.1", "10.0.0.5", "169.254.169.254", "192.168.1.1"):
        assert not is_public_url("http://internal/x.ics", fake_resolver(ip))


def test_non_http_scheme_rejected():
    assert not is_public_url("file:///etc/passwd", fake_resolver("8.8.8.8"))


def test_unresolvable_host_rejected():
    def boom(*a, **k):
        raise OSError("nope")

    assert not is_public_url("https://nope.invalid/", boom)

===== FILE: backend/tests/test_store.py =====
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

import pytest

from app.models import (
    Count,
    EventQuery,
    EventUpdate,
    Listener,
    ListenerRecord,
    Notification,
    EventRecord,
    BoundingBox,
    Location
)
from app.seed import seed
from app.store.sqlite import SqliteStore, to_iso


def uris(page):
    return [e.uri.rsplit("/", 1)[1] for e in page.events]


def test_upsert_created_updated_unchanged(store, make_event):
    ev = make_event()
    assert store.upsert_event(ev) == "created"
    assert store.upsert_event(ev) == "unchanged"
    assert store.upsert_event(ev.model_copy(update={"cid": "bafy2"})) == "updated"
    changed = make_event(name="Renamed", cid="bafy2")
    assert store.upsert_event(changed) == "updated"
    assert store.get_event(ev.uri).record.name == "Renamed"


def test_update_keeps_original_indexed_at(store, make_event, now):
    store.upsert_event(make_event())
    later = make_event(name="New", cid="bafy2", indexed_at=now + timedelta(hours=1))
    store.upsert_event(later)
    assert store.get_event(later.uri).indexed_at == now


def test_get_event_roundtrip(store, make_event):
    ev = make_event(tags=["food"], group="ACM", author_handle="a.bsky.social")
    store.upsert_event(ev)
    assert store.get_event(ev.uri) == ev
    assert store.get_event("at://nope") is None


def test_delete_event(store, make_event):
    ev = make_event(tags=["food"])
    store.upsert_event(ev)
    store.subscribe("subscriber1", ev.uri)
    assert store.delete_event(ev.uri) is True
    assert store.subscribers_of(ev.uri) == []
    assert store.delete_event(ev.uri) is False
    assert store.get_event(ev.uri) is None
    assert store.list_tags(datetime.now(UTC)) == []


def test_query_hides_expired_and_orders(store, make_event, now):
    store.upsert_event(make_event("b", start_h=2, end_h=3))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("a", start_h=2, end_h=3))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("c", start_h=-1, end_h=1))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("old", start_h=-3, end_h=-2))
    assert uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(), now)) == ["c", "a", "b"]
    q = EventQuery(ends_after=now - timedelta(hours=5))
    assert uris(store.query_events(q, now)) == ["old", "c", "a", "b"]


def test_query_filters(store, make_event, now):
    store.upsert_event(make_event("1", tags=["food", "free"], group="ACM", name="Pizza Night"))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("2", tags=["music"], description="Live JAZZ band"))
    store.upsert_event(
        make_event("3", tags=["tech"], location="Geisel Library", start_h=10, end_h=11)
    )
    assert uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(tags=["music", "free"]), now)) == ["1", "2"]
    assert uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(tags=["#Food"]), now)) == ["1"]
    assert uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(group="ACM"), now)) == ["1"]
    assert uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(group="acm"), now)) == []
    assert uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(text="pizza"), now)) == ["1"]
    assert uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(text="jazz"), now)) == ["2"]
    assert uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(text="geisel"), now)) == ["3"]
    before = EventQuery(starts_before=now + timedelta(hours=5))
    assert uris(store.query_events(before, now)) == ["1", "2"]


def test_query_pagination(store, make_event, now):
    for i in range(5):
        store.upsert_event(make_event(f"e{i}", start_h=1 + i // 2, end_h=10))
    seen: list[str] = []
    q = EventQuery(limit=2)
    while True:
        page = store.query_events(q, now)
        seen += uris(page)
        if page.cursor is None:
            break
        q = q.model_copy(update={"cursor": page.cursor})
    assert seen == ["e0", "e1", "e2", "e3", "e4"]


def test_query_exact_page_has_no_cursor(store, make_event, now):
    store.upsert_event(make_event("a"))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("b"))
    assert store.query_events(EventQuery(limit=2), now).cursor is None


def test_query_bad_cursor(store, now):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        store.query_events(EventQuery(cursor="!!!not-base64"), now)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        store.query_events(EventQuery(cursor="bm9waXBl"), now)  # "nopipe"


def test_naive_datetimes_are_utc(store, make_event, now):
    store.upsert_event(make_event("a", start_h=-1, end_h=1))
    naive_now = now.astimezone(UTC).replace(tzinfo=None)
    assert uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(), naive_now)) == ["a"]
    assert to_iso(naive_now) == to_iso(now)


def test_delete_expired_cascades(store, make_event, now):
    old = make_event("old", start_h=-3, end_h=-1, tags=["food"])
    live = make_event("live", start_h=-1, end_h=1, tags=["food"])
    store.upsert_event(old)
    store.upsert_event(live)
    store.subscribe("subscriber1", old.uri)
    store.subscribe("subscriber1", live.uri)
    store.add_update(EventUpdate(event_uri=old.uri, text="hi", created_at=now))
    assert store.delete_expired(now) == [old.uri]
    assert store.get_event(old.uri) is None
    assert store.list_updates(old.uri) == []
    assert store.subscriptions_of("subscriber1") == [live.uri]
    assert store.list_tags(now - timedelta(hours=5)) == [Count(value="food", count=1)]


def test_list_tags_and_groups(store, make_event, now):
    store.upsert_event(make_event("1", tags=["food", "free"], group="ACM"))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("2", tags=["free", "music"], group="KSDT"))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("3", tags=["free"], group="ACM"))
    store.upsert_event(make_event("x", tags=["zzz"], group="Gone", start_h=-3, end_h=-2))
    assert store.list_tags(now) == [
        Count(value="free", count=3),
        Count(value="food", count=1),
        Count(value="music", count=1),
    ]
    assert store.list_groups(now) == [Count(value="ACM", count=2), Count(value="KSDT", count=1)]


def test_updates(store, now):
    store.add_update(EventUpdate(event_uri="u", text="2nd", created_at=now + timedelta(1)))
    store.add_update(EventUpdate(event_uri="u", text="1st", source_uri="at://p", created_at=now))
    got = store.list_updates("u")
    assert [u.text for u in got] == ["1st", "2nd"]
    assert got[0].source_uri == "at://p"
    assert got[0].created_at == now


def test_listeners(store, now):
    rec = ListenerRecord(subject="at://did:a/x/1", hashtag="#Party", created_at=now)
    store.upsert_listener(Listener(uri="at://did:a/l/1", author_did="did:a", record=rec))
    store.upsert_listener(Listener(uri="at://did:b/l/1", author_did="did:b", record=rec))
    assert store.listener_dids() == ["did:a", "did:b"]
    [got] = store.listeners_for_did("did:a")
    assert got.record.hashtag == "party"
    assert store.delete_listener("at://did:a/l/1") is True
    assert store.delete_listener("at://did:a/l/1") is False
    assert store.listener_dids() == ["did:b"]


def test_subscriptions(store):
    store.subscribe("sub-a", "at://e1")
    store.subscribe("sub-a", "at://e1")
    store.subscribe("sub-b", "at://e1")
    store.subscribe("sub-a", "at://e2")
    assert store.subscribers_of("at://e1") == ["sub-a", "sub-b"]
    assert store.subscriptions_of("sub-a") == ["at://e1", "at://e2"]
    store.unsubscribe("sub-a", "at://e1")
    assert store.subscribers_of("at://e1") == ["sub-b"]


def test_notifications(store, now):
    def make(sub: str) -> Notification:
        return Notification(
            subscriber_id=sub, event_uri="at://e", kind="updated", message="m", created_at=now
        )

    a1 = store.add_notification(make("a"))
    store.add_notification(make("b"))
    a2 = store.add_notification(make("a"))
    assert a1.id is not None and a2.id > a1.id
    assert store.list_notifications("a") == [a1, a2]
    assert store.list_notifications("a", since_id=a1.id) == [a2]


def test_cursors(store):
    assert store.get_cursor("jetstream") is None
    store.set_cursor("jetstream", 5)
    store.set_cursor("jetstream", 7)
    assert store.get_cursor("jetstream") == 7


def test_file_store_persists(tmp_path, make_event):
    path = str(tmp_path / "sub" / "db.sqlite")
    s = SqliteStore(path)
    s.upsert_event(make_event())
    s.close()
    s2 = SqliteStore(path)
    assert s2.get_event(make_event().uri) is not None
    s2.close()


def test_seed(store, now):
    assert seed(store, now) == 12
    page = store.query_events(EventQuery(limit=100), now)
    assert len(page.events) == 12
    live = [e for e in page.events if e.record.starts_at <= now <= e.record.ends_at]
    assert len(live) >= 2
    assert any(e.record.ends_at - now <= timedelta(hours=1) for e in live)
    assert all(e.record.starts_at < now + timedelta(days=7) for e in page.events)

def test_query_sort_newest_and_pagination(store, make_event, now):
    for i in range(5):
        ev = make_event(f"e{i}", start_h=10 - i, end_h=20)
        rec = ev.record.model_copy(update={"created_at": now + timedelta(minutes=i)})
        store.upsert_event(ev.model_copy(update={"record": rec}))

    seen: list[str] = []
    q = EventQuery(sort="newest", limit=2)
    while True:
        page = store.query_events(q, now)
        seen += uris(page)
        if page.cursor is None:
            break
        q = q.model_copy(update={"cursor": page.cursor})
    assert seen == ["e4", "e3", "e2", "e1", "e0"]

    # Cursor from one sort mode is rejected for the other
    p = store.query_events(EventQuery(sort="soonest", limit=1), now)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        store.query_events(EventQuery(sort="newest", cursor=p.cursor), now)


def test_query_bbox_and_antimeridian(store, make_event, now):
    def at_pos(rkey: str, lat: str | None, lng: str | None):
        ev = make_event(rkey)
        rec = ev.record.model_copy(update={"location": Location(name=rkey, lat=lat, lng=lng)})
        store.upsert_event(ev.model_copy(update={"record": rec}))

    at_pos("ucsd", "32.88", "-117.24")
    at_pos("downtown", "32.71", "-117.16")
    at_pos("nocoords", None, None)
    at_pos("fiji_e", "-18.0", "178.0")
    at_pos("fiji_w", "-18.0", "-178.0")

    campus = BoundingBox(west=-117.26, south=32.86, east=-117.22, north=32.90)
    assert uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(bbox=campus), now)) == ["ucsd"]

    dateline = BoundingBox(west=175.0, south=-20.0, east=-175.0, north=-15.0)
    assert set(uris(store.query_events(EventQuery(bbox=dateline), now))) == {"fiji_e", "fiji_w"}
===== FILE: backend/tests/test_subscriptions.py =====
import asyncio

from app.api.subscriptions import sse_messages
from app.models import Notification

SUB = "subscriber-123"


def _body(uri: str, sub: str = SUB) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"subscriberId": sub, "eventUri": uri}


def test_subscribe_list_unsubscribe(client, store, make_event):
    ev = make_event()
    store.upsert_event(ev)
    assert client.post("/api/subscriptions", json=_body(ev.uri)).status_code == 204
    assert client.get("/api/subscriptions", params={"subscriberId": SUB}).json() == [ev.uri]
    resp = client.request("DELETE", "/api/subscriptions", json=_body(ev.uri))
    assert resp.status_code == 204
    assert client.get("/api/subscriptions", params={"subscriberId": SUB}).json() == []


def test_subscribe_unknown_event_404(client):
    assert client.post("/api/subscriptions", json=_body("at://nope")).status_code == 404


def test_subscriber_id_validation(client, store, make_event):
    ev = make_event()
    store.upsert_event(ev)
    for bad in ["short", "x" * 65, "has space!"]:
        assert client.post("/api/subscriptions", json=_body(ev.uri, bad)).status_code == 422
        assert client.get("/api/subscriptions", params={"subscriberId": bad}).status_code == 422
    assert client.get("/api/notifications").status_code == 422


async def test_publish_persists_and_lists(client, store, notifier, make_event):
    ev = make_event()
    store.upsert_event(ev)
    store.subscribe(SUB, ev.uri)
    store.subscribe("other-subscriber", ev.uri)
    assert await notifier.publish(ev.uri, "updated", "Time changed") == 2
    assert await notifier.publish("at://no-subs", "updated", "x") == 0

    body = client.get("/api/notifications", params={"subscriberId": SUB}).json()
    assert len(body) == 1
    n = body[0]
    assert (n["subscriberId"], n["eventUri"], n["kind"], n["message"]) == (
        SUB, ev.uri, "updated", "Time changed",
    )  # fmt: skip
    since = client.get("/api/notifications", params={"subscriberId": SUB, "since": n["id"]})
    assert since.json() == []


async def test_publish_pushes_to_all_tabs(store, notifier):
    store.subscribe(SUB, "at://e")
    tab1, tab2 = notifier.connect(SUB), notifier.connect(SUB)
    await notifier.publish("at://e", "announcement", "Doors open")
    assert tab1.get_nowait().message == tab2.get_nowait().message == "Doors open"
    notifier.disconnect(SUB, tab1)
    await notifier.publish("at://e", "announcement", "Second")
    assert tab1.empty()
    assert tab2.get_nowait().message == "Second"
    notifier.disconnect(SUB, tab2)
    notifier.disconnect(SUB, tab2)  # idempotent


async def test_sse_messages_stream(store, notifier):
    store.subscribe(SUB, "at://e")
    disconnected = False

    async def is_disconnected() -> bool:
        return disconnected

    gen = sse_messages(notifier, SUB, is_disconnected, ping_interval_s=0.01)
    assert await anext(gen) == ": connected\n\n"
    assert await anext(gen) == ": ping\n\n"

    await notifier.publish("at://e", "deleted", "Cancelled")
    frame = await anext(gen)
    lines = frame.split("\n")
    assert lines[0].startswith("id: ")
    n = Notification.model_validate_json(lines[1].removeprefix("data: "))
    assert (n.kind, n.message, n.subscriber_id) == ("deleted", "Cancelled", SUB)

    disconnected = True
    await asyncio.sleep(0.02)
    assert [f async for f in gen] in ([], [": ping\n\n"])
    assert notifier._queues == {}


async def test_sse_releases_queue_on_close(notifier):
    async def never() -> bool:
        return False

    gen = sse_messages(notifier, SUB, never, ping_interval_s=10)
    await anext(gen)
    assert SUB in notifier._queues
    await gen.aclose()
    assert notifier._queues == {}


def test_stream_endpoint_validates(client):
    resp = client.get("/api/notifications/stream", params={"subscriberId": "bad"})
    assert resp.status_code == 422

from datetime import timedelta

from app.views import event_view


def test_event_view_shape(make_event, now):

    view = event_view(ev, now)
    assert set(view) == {
        "uri", "cid", "authorDid", "authorHandle", "source", "name", "description",
        "startsAt", "endsAt", "location", "tags", "group", "sourceUrl", "indexedAt",
        "updatedAt", "status", "createdAt",
    }  # fmt: skip
    assert view["authorHandle"] == "acm.bsky.social"
    assert view["location"] == {
        "name": "Price Center", "address": None, "lat": "32.8797", "lng": "-117.2362",
    }  # fmt: skip

def test_status(make_event, now):
    assert event_view(make_event(start_h=1, end_h=2), now)["status"] == "upcoming"
    assert event_view(make_event(start_h=-1, end_h=2), now)["status"] == "live"
    assert event_view(make_event(start_h=-1, end_h=0.5), now)["status"] == "ending_soon"
    assert event_view(make_event(start_h=0, end_h=1), now)["status"] == "ending_soon"


def test_status_naive_now(make_event, now):
    naive = (now + timedelta(minutes=1)).replace(tzinfo=None)
    assert event_view(make_event(start_h=0, end_h=3), naive)["status"] == "live"

===== FILE: docs/ARCHITECTURE.md =====
# Architecture

## Infrastructure

```mermaid
flowchart LR
  subgraph Browser["Student's browser (vanilla JS PWA)"]
    UI["List / Map / Compose views"]
    SW["Service worker<br/>offline cache"]
    GEO["Geolocation<br/>(never leaves device)"]
  end

  subgraph ATP["AT Protocol network"]
    PDS[("User's PDS<br/>bsky.social or self-hosted")]
    RELAY["Relay"]
    JS["Jetstream<br/>(JSON firehose)"]
    PLC["plc.directory<br/>DID → handle"]
  end

  subgraph Host["Our container (Fly / Render free tier)"]
    API["FastAPI AppView<br/>/api/*  +  static frontend"]
    ING["Ingest<br/>Jetstream client · CommitProcessor"]
    ICAL["iCal poller"]
    EXP["Expiry sweeper"]
    NOTIF["Notifier<br/>SSE fan-out"]
    DB[("SQLite index<br/>events · tags · subs · notifications")]
  end

  EXT["External calendars (.ics)"]

  UI -- "createRecord / putRecord<br/>(event, listener)" --> PDS
  PDS --> RELAY --> JS
  JS -- "WebSocket: event + listener records<br/>posts only from listener DIDs" --> ING
  ING --> DB
  ING -.-> PLC
  ICAL -- "HTTP poll" --> EXT
  ICAL --> DB
  EXP --> DB
  ING & ICAL & EXP --> NOTIF --> DB
  UI -- "GET /api/events, /tags, /groups" --> API --> DB
  NOTIF -- "SSE /api/notifications/stream" --> UI
  SW -.caches.- API
```

## How data moves

1. **Write.** The host's browser writes an `app.communitymap.event` record straight to their PDS. Our server never holds their credentials.
2. **Index.** The PDS passes the record to the relay and then to Jetstream. `JetstreamClient` sends it to `CommitProcessor`, which validates it against the lexicon and upserts it into SQLite. A cursor is saved so a restart picks up where it left off.
3. **Notify.** If an update changes something people can see (time, place, name), the processor builds a readable diff and sends it to everyone subscribed. They get it on the SSE stream, and it is also saved so they can catch up after going offline.
4. **Listeners (Host Henry).** A host can attach a listener record (event + hashtag) to an event. When that host posts on Bluesky with the hashtag, the post becomes an announcement on the event. We only subscribe to posts from accounts that have a listener, never the full firehose.
5. **Expire.** Each sweep tells subscribers that the event has ended, then deletes it from the index. The record stays on the user's PDS, since that data belongs to them.

## Code layout (each box is one team's area)

```
lexicons/            record schemas (the public contract on the network)
backend/app/
  models.py          shared Pydantic models (contract)
  store/base.py      EventStore Protocol (contract) → sqlite.py
  notify/base.py     Notifier Protocol (contract)   → service.py (SSE)
  ingest/            jetstream.py · processor.py · ical.py · handles.py · netguard.py
  api/               meta · events · subscriptions · imports · dev
  expiry.py seed.py views.py
  main.py            composition root (the only file that knows concrete classes)
frontend/src/
  api.js cache.js store.js filters.js geo.js subscriptions.js
  atproto.js devWriter.js   (same interface, so writers can be swapped)
  views/             listView · mapView · detailView · composeView · filterBar · loginView
  app.js             wiring
```

Modules only depend on the interfaces. Swapping SQLite for Postgres, Jetstream for another feed, or Leaflet tiles for Overture only touches one file plus `main.py`.

===== FILE: docs/CONTRACTS.md =====
# Contracts

The interfaces every module builds against. Change these only by agreement.

| Contract | File |
|---|---|
| Record schemas (on users' PDS) | `lexicons/*.json` |
| Domain models | `backend/app/models.py` |
| Storage interface | `backend/app/store/base.py` |
| Notifier interface | `backend/app/notify/base.py` |
| HTTP API | this file |

## HTTP API (JSON, camelCase)

`EventView` = `IndexedEvent` fields flattened: `{uri, cid, authorDid, authorHandle, source,
name, description, startsAt, endsAt, location:{name,address,lat,lng}, tags, group, sourceUrl,
createdAt, indexedAt, updatedAt, status}` where `status` is `upcoming | live | ending_soon` (ending within 1h).

| Method | Path | Body / Query | Returns |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/health` | | `{ok:true}` |
| GET | `/api/config` | | `{devMode, jetstreamEnabled, eventNsid, listenerNsid}` |
| GET | `/api/events` | `tag (repeatable) / tags (comma-separated or repeatable, match any), group, q, before, after (ISO), bbox (west,south,east,north), sort (soonest\newest), limit, cursor` | `{events:[EventView], cursor} (sets ETag + Cache-Control: no-cache; honors If-None-Match → 304)` |
| GET | `/api/event` | `uri` | `EventView & {updates:[{text,sourceUri,createdAt}]}` or 404 |
| GET | `/api/tags` | | `[{value,count}]` |
| GET | `/api/groups` | | `[{value,count}]` |
| POST | `/api/subscriptions` | `{subscriberId, eventUri}` | 204 |
| DELETE | `/api/subscriptions` | `{subscriberId, eventUri}` | 204 |
| GET | `/api/subscriptions` | `subscriberId` | `[eventUri]` |
| GET | `/api/notifications` | `subscriberId`, `since` (id) | `[Notification]` |
| GET | `/api/notifications/stream` | `subscriberId` | SSE, `data: <Notification JSON>` per message |
| POST | `/api/import/ical` | `{url}` | `{imported:n}` |
| POST | `/api/dev/records` | Jetstream-shaped commit JSON (dev mode only) | `{ok:true}` |

Location privacy: the API never receives the user's position. "Near me" sorting happens in
the browser.

## Writes

Clients write directly to the user's PDS (`com.atproto.repo.createRecord` / `putRecord` /
`deleteRecord`) with collection `app.communitymap.event` or `app.communitymap.listener`.
The backend only *reads* the network (Jetstream) and builds the index. In dev mode the
frontend posts the same commit shape to `/api/dev/records` instead.

## Jetstream commit shape (input to ingest, and to `/api/dev/records`)

```json
{"did":"did:plc:abc","time_us":1731300000000000,"kind":"commit",
 "commit":{"operation":"create|update|delete","collection":"app.communitymap.event",
           "rkey":"3k...","cid":"bafy...","record":{...}}}
```

===== FILE: docs/DECISIONS.md =====
# Design decisions to debate

These are the choices made in this prototype that go beyond the ADRs, with the main argument against each.

1. **The backend is a read-only AppView. Clients write to their own PDS.**
   *For:* the data really belongs to users, we hold no credentials, and the design follows AT Protocol conventions.
   *Against:* new events take Relay → Jetstream time (usually about a second) to show up, and we can't moderate them before they are published.

2. **Subscriptions are anonymous and server-side (a random `subscriberId` per browser), not records in the PDS.**
   *For:* subscriptions stay private (PDS records are public), and no login is needed to follow an event.
   *Against:* they don't follow you across devices, and they can't be moved to another AppView.

3. **SSE instead of Web Push.**
   *For:* no keys and no push service. It works on every browser, and missed notifications are caught up on reconnect.
   *Against:* nothing arrives while the tab is closed. Web Push (VAPID) is the next step for the Larry persona.

4. **SQLite in a single container.**
   *For:* free, nothing to operate, fast enough for one campus. The index can be rebuilt by replaying Jetstream.
   *Against:* no horizontal scaling. `EventStore` is a Protocol, so moving to Postgres means writing one new file.

5. **Expiry deletes the event from the index, not from the PDS.**
   *For:* we can't delete user data, and it shouldn't be ours to delete.
   *Against:* expired records stay on the network, and another AppView might still show them.

6. **Listeners match a hashtag on the host's own posts.**
   *For:* simple, and it can't be spoofed (the listener has to be owned by the same DID as the event).
   *Against:* the post text becomes an announcement but doesn't change the event's fields. Structured sync, such as "post says the room changed", would need parsing or an LLM.

7. **"Near me" is computed in the browser.**
   *For:* we never see or store anyone's location, which addresses the location risk in §5.3.
   *Against:* we can't do a server-side radius query, so the client re-sorts only the pages it has already loaded.

8. **Lat/lng are optional strings in the lexicon.**
   *For:* lexicons have no float type, and the list-first ADR means coordinates aren't required.
   *Against:* events without coordinates don't appear on the map. Changing a lexicon later is costly, so this is worth deciding now.

## Known gaps

- **Login uses Bluesky app passwords, not OAuth.** OAuth is the right long-term choice but needs a hosted client-metadata document.
- **There is no moderation or trust score yet.** We rely on Bluesky's account-level moderation.
- **The iCal guard can be bypassed by DNS rebinding.** Pin the resolved IP before production.
- **Bluesky login and the map tab aren't covered by the Playwright tests.** The tests use demo mode and simulate Bluesky posts with dev commits, so real login and the map are still unverified.

===== FILE: docs/TEAM.md =====
# How the work was split

The architect wrote the contracts first. Three engineers (Claude sub-agents) then built against those contracts in parallel. Each one owned a separate set of directories, so nobody blocked anyone else and there were no merge conflicts.

| Phase | Owner | Deliverable |
|---|---|---|
| 0. Contracts | Architect | `lexicons/`, `models.py`, `store/base.py`, `notify/base.py`, `docs/CONTRACTS.md`, repo + CI skeleton |
| 1a. Backend Core | Engineer A | SQLite store, API routers (events, subscriptions, SSE), notifier, expiry, seed data. 41 tests |
| 1b. Ingest | Engineer B | Jetstream client, commit processor + diffs, listeners, handle resolver, iCal import, dev route. 50 tests |
| 1c. Frontend | Engineer C | Vanilla-JS PWA: list/map/detail/compose views, PDS writer, demo writer, subscriptions, service worker. 81 tests |
| 2. Integration | Architect | `main.py`, end-to-end test, SSRF guard, safe defaults, Docker, docs, live checks (browser, SSE, real Jetstream) |

**Why it split this way.** The seams follow the data flow (network → index → API → UI). Each one is a Python `Protocol` or an HTTP contract. Every engineer tested against fakes of the other engineers' interfaces (for example `tests/ingest_fakes.py`), so all three could work at the same time.

**Found during integration and fixed.** Three issues came up:
- **EventView had no `createdAt`.** The Frontend needed it to edit events, so it was added.
- **The iCal import could fetch internal URLs (SSRF).** Ingest flagged this, and `netguard.py` now blocks private hosts.
- **`dev_mode` defaulted to on.** It is now off by default.

**How a human team could map to this split.** The spec lists eight developers. Lead A (backend) could take Core + Ingest with four devs. Lead B (frontend) could take the views + offline/PWA work with four devs. The contracts in Phase 0 are owned by the leads together.

import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

const PORT = 8100;

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: './tests',
  fullyParallel: false, // stories share one seeded backend
  workers: 1,
  retries: process.env.CI ? 1 : 0,
  reporter: process.env.CI ? [['list'], ['html', { open: 'never' }]] : 'list',
  use: {
    baseURL: `http://localhost:${PORT}`,
    serviceWorkers: 'block', // the offline story opts back in
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
  },
  projects: [
    { name: 'desktop', use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'] } },
    { name: 'mobile', use: { ...devices['Pixel 7'] }, testMatch: /discover|mobile/ },
  ],
  webServer: {
    command: './serve.sh',
    url: `http://localhost:${PORT}/api/health`,
    reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,
    env: { PORT: String(PORT) },
  },
});

===== FILE: e2e/serve.sh =====
#!/usr/bin/env sh
# Starts a fresh, seeded backend for e2e runs (demo mode on, no live network).
set -e
cd "$(dirname "$0")/../backend"
PY="${PYTHON:-../.venv/bin/python}"
DB="$(mktemp -d)/e2e.db"
export CM_DB_PATH="$DB" CM_DEV_MODE=true CM_JETSTREAM_ENABLED=false CM_EXPIRY_INTERVAL_S=2
"$PY" -m app.seed
exec "$PY" -m uvicorn app.main:app --port "${PORT:-8100}"

===== FILE: e2e/tests/discover.spec.js =====
// Story: a new/transfer student browses what's happening on campus and filters by interest.
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import { card, openFeed } from './helpers.js';

test.describe('Student discovers events', () => {
  test('feed is grouped by day, soonest first', async ({ page }) => {
    await openFeed(page);
    await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Today', level: 2 })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(page.locator('article.card').first()).toContainText('Late Night Study Jam');
  });

  test('filter by tag shows only matching events', async ({ page }) => {
    await openFeed(page);
    await page.getByRole('search').getByRole('button', { name: '#music' }).click();
    const cards = page.locator('article.card');
    await expect(cards.first()).toBeVisible();
    for (const tags of await cards.locator('ul.chips').allInnerTexts()) {
      expect(tags).toContain('#music');
    }
  });

  test('text search narrows the feed', async ({ page }) => {
    await openFeed(page);
    await page.getByPlaceholder('Search events').fill('rust');
    await expect(page.locator('article.card')).toHaveCount(1);
    await expect(card(page, 'Intro to Rust Workshop')).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('opening an event shows its details', async ({ page }) => {
    await openFeed(page);
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Engineering Career Fair' }).click();
    const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog');
    await expect(dialog.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Engineering Career Fair' })).toBeVisible();
    await expect(dialog.getByText('Updates')).toBeVisible();
  });

  test('layout fits the screen without horizontal scrolling', async ({ page }) => {
    await openFeed(page);
    const overflow = await page.evaluate(
      () => document.documentElement.scrollWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth,
    );
    expect(overflow).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
  });
});

===== FILE: e2e/tests/expiry.spec.js =====
// Story: posted events expire gracefully instead of cluttering the feed.
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import { card, commit, inHours, openFeed } from './helpers.js';

test('an event ending within the hour is flagged "Ending soon"', async ({ page }) => {
  await openFeed(page);
  await expect(card(page, 'Late Night Study Jam').getByText('Ending soon')).toBeVisible();
});

test('an ended event disappears from the feed', async ({ page, request }) => {
  const name = `Flash Sale ${Date.now()}`;
  const record = (endsAt) => ({
    $type: 'app.communitymap.event', name, startsAt: inHours(-2), endsAt,
    location: { name: 'Library Walk' }, createdAt: inHours(-3),
  });
  await commit(request, 'did:plc:shop', 'app.communitymap.event', 'create', 'sale1', record(inHours(1)));
  await openFeed(page);
  await expect(card(page, name)).toBeVisible();

  // The host moves the end time into the past; the sweeper removes it.
  await commit(request, 'did:plc:shop', 'app.communitymap.event', 'update', 'sale1', record(inHours(-0.01)));
  await expect(async () => {
    await page.reload();
    await page.locator('article.card').first().waitFor();
    await expect(card(page, name)).toHaveCount(0);
  }).toPass({ timeout: 10_000 });
});

===== FILE: e2e/tests/helpers.js =====
/** Shared helpers for user-story specs. */

/** Card <article> for an event by name. */
export const card = (page, name) => page.locator('article.card', { hasText: name });

/** Wait until the feed has rendered at least one card. */
export async function openFeed(page) {
  await page.goto('/');
  await page.locator('article.card').first().waitFor();
}

/** ISO string `hours` from now. */
export const inHours = (hours) => new Date(Date.now() + hours * 3_600_000).toISOString();

/** datetime-local value (local time, minutes precision) `hours` from now. */
export function localInput(hours) {
  const d = new Date(Date.now() + hours * 3_600_000);
  const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}T${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
}

/** Fill and submit the "New event" dialog in demo mode. */
export async function createEvent(page, { name, location, tags = '', hashtag = '' }) {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'New event' }).click();
  const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog');
  await dialog.getByLabel('Event name').fill(name);
  await dialog.getByLabel('Starts').fill(localInput(1));
  await dialog.getByLabel('Ends').fill(localInput(3));
  await dialog.getByLabel('Location name').fill(location);
  if (tags) await dialog.getByLabel('Tags').fill(tags);
  if (hashtag) await dialog.getByLabel(/Bluesky hashtag/).fill(hashtag);
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create event' }).click();
  await card(page, name).waitFor();
}

/** Simulate a Jetstream commit (what the real firehose would deliver). */
export async function commit(request, did, collection, operation, rkey, record) {
  const res = await request.post('/api/dev/records', {
    data: { did, time_us: Date.now() * 1000, kind: 'commit',
      commit: { operation, collection, rkey, cid: `cid-${Date.now()}`, record } },
  });
  if (!res.ok()) throw new Error(`commit failed: ${res.status()}`);
}

/** Read the demo DID the browser is posting as. */
export const demoDid = (page) => page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem('cm:demo-did'));

// Story (Host Henry): he announces on Bluesky and the event listing stays in sync automatically.
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import { card, commit, createEvent, demoDid, openFeed } from './helpers.js';

test("Henry's Bluesky post with the event hashtag appears as an update", async ({ page, browser, request }) => {
  const name = `Henry's Hack Night ${Date.now()}`;
  const hashtag = `hack${Date.now()}`;

  await openFeed(page);
  await createEvent(page, { name, location: 'CSE Building', tags: 'tech', hashtag });
  const did = await demoDid(page);
  expect(did).toMatch(/^did:plc:demo-/);

  // A student subscribes to Henry's event.
  const student = await (await browser.newContext()).newPage();
  await openFeed(student);
  await student.getByPlaceholder('Search events').fill(name);
  await card(student, name).getByRole('button', { name: 'Subscribe' }).click();
  await expect(card(student, name).getByRole('button', { name: 'Subscribed' })).toBeVisible();

  // Henry posts on Bluesky (delivered via the firehose; simulated here).
  const text = `Pizza just arrived, come by room 1202! #${hashtag}`;
  await commit(request, did, 'app.bsky.feed.post', 'create', `post${Date.now()}`, {
    $type: 'app.bsky.feed.post', text, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  });

  await expect(student.locator('#toasts')).toContainText('Pizza just arrived', { timeout: 10_000 });

  await student.getByRole('button', { name }).click();
  await expect(student.getByRole('dialog').getByText(text)).toBeVisible();
});

  const name = `Henry's Board Games ${Date.now()}`;
  const hashtag = `games${Date.now()}`;

  await createEvent(page, { name, location: 'Price Center', hashtag });

  await commit(request, 'did:plc:stranger', 'app.bsky.feed.post', 'create', 'p1', {
    text: `Event cancelled lol #${hashtag}`, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  });

  await page.getByRole('button', { name }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('dialog').getByText('No announcements yet.')).toBeVisible();
});

// Story (Last-minute Larry): he moves his party to a new spot; subscribed attendees hear about it fast.
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import { card, createEvent, openFeed } from './helpers.js';

test('attendee gets a live notice when Larry changes the location', async ({ browser }) => {
  const larry = await (await browser.newContext()).newPage();
  const attendee = await (await browser.newContext()).newPage();
  const name = `Larry's Picnic ${Date.now()}`;

  await openFeed(larry);
  await createEvent(larry, { name, location: 'Mission Bay Park', tags: 'social, food' });

  await openFeed(attendee);
  await attendee.getByPlaceholder('Search events').fill(name);
  await card(attendee, name).getByRole('button', { name: 'Subscribe' }).click();
  await expect(card(attendee, name).getByRole('button', { name: 'Subscribed' })).toBeVisible();

  // Larry edits the event: the tables were taken, moving to Kellogg Park.
  await larry.getByRole('button', { name }).click();
  await larry.getByRole('dialog').getByRole('button', { name: 'Edit' }).click();
  const form = larry.getByRole('dialog').filter({ has: larry.getByLabel('Location name') });
  await form.getByLabel('Location name').fill('Kellogg Park');
  await form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save changes' }).click();

  await expect(attendee.locator('#toasts')).toContainText('Kellogg Park', { timeout: 10_000 });

  // The feed reflects the change after a refresh, flagged as updated.
  await attendee.reload();
  await attendee.getByPlaceholder('Search events').fill(name);
  await expect(card(attendee, name)).toContainText('Kellogg Park');
  await expect(card(attendee, name).getByText('Updated')).toBeVisible();
});

// Story: a student on bad campus WiFi can still see events they loaded earlier.
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
import { openFeed } from './helpers.js';

test.use({ serviceWorkers: 'allow' });

test('feed still loads from cache when the network drops', async ({ page, context }) => {
  await openFeed(page);
  await page.evaluate(() => navigator.serviceWorker.ready);
  await page.reload(); // let the service worker control the page and cache /api responses
  await page.locator('article.card').first().waitFor();

  await context.setOffline(true);
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.locator('article.card', { hasText: 'Late Night Study Jam' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator('.notice--stale')).toBeVisible();
});

===== FILE: exportissues.py =====

import json
import subprocess
import re

def get_issues():
    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            "gh", "issue", "list",
            "--state", "all",
            "--limit", "1000",
            "--json",
            "number,title,body,state,labels,createdAt,updatedAt"
        ],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=True
    )
    return json.loads(result.stdout)


def anonymize(text):
    if not text:
        return ""

    # Remove email addresses
    text = re.sub(
        r'\b[\w.+-]+@[\w.-]+\.[a-zA-Z]{2,}\b',
        '[EMAIL REDACTED]', text
    )

    # Remove GitHub usernames
    text = re.sub(
        r'(?<![\w/])@[A-Za-z0-9-]+',
        '[USER REDACTED]', text
    )

    return text


def main():
    issues = get_issues()
    output = []

    for index, issue in enumerate(issues, 1):
        output.append({
            "id": f"ISSUE-{index}",
            "title": anonymize(issue["title"]),
            "body": anonymize(issue["body"]),
            "state": issue["state"],
            "labels": [
                label["name"]
                for label in issue["labels"]
            ],
            "created_at": issue["createdAt"],
            "updated_at": issue["updatedAt"]
        })

    with open(
        "issues_anonymized.json",
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:
        json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

    print(f"Exported {len(output)} anonymized issues.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
