/**
 * Accessible toast messages rendered into an aria-live region.
 * @module toast
 */
import { h } from './dom.js';

/**
 * @param {HTMLElement} region element with aria-live="polite"
 * @param {{timeoutMs?: number}} [opts]
 * @returns {(message: string, opts?: {kind?: 'info'|'error'}) => void}
 */
export function createToaster(region, { timeoutMs = 5000 } = {}) {
  return (message, { kind = 'info' } = {}) => {
    const el = h('div', { class: `toast toast--${kind}` }, message);
    region.append(el);
    setTimeout(() => el.remove(), timeoutMs);
  };
}

===== FILE: frontend/src/util.js =====
/**
 * Small generic utilities.
 * @module util
 */

/**
 * Debounce a function.
 * @template {(...a: any[]) => void} F
 * @param {F} fn
 * @param {number} ms
 * @returns {F & {cancel: () => void}}
 */
export function debounce(fn, ms) {
  let t;
  const wrapped = (...args) => {
    clearTimeout(t);
    t = setTimeout(() => fn(...args), ms);
  };
  wrapped.cancel = () => clearTimeout(t);
  return wrapped;
}

/**
 * Random lowercase base32-ish id (uses crypto when available).
 * @param {number} [len=16]
 * @returns {string}
 */
export function randomId(len = 16) {
  const alphabet = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz234567';
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(len);
  if (globalThis.crypto?.getRandomValues) globalThis.crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  else for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) bytes[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
  return Array.from(bytes, (b) => alphabet[b % 32]).join('');
}

/**
 * Safely obtain a Web Storage object (access itself can throw in private modes).
 * @param {'localStorage'|'sessionStorage'} name
 * @returns {Storage|null}
 */
export function getStorage(name) {
  try {
    return globalThis[name] ?? null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Storage read that never throws.
 * @param {Storage|null} storage
 * @param {string} key
 * @returns {string|null}
 */
export function storageGet(storage, key) {
  try {
    return storage ? storage.getItem(key) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Storage write that never throws. Returns false if it failed.
 * @param {Storage|null} storage
 * @param {string} key
 * @param {string|null} value null removes the key
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
export function storageSet(storage, key, value) {
  try {
    if (!storage) return false;
    if (value == null) storage.removeItem(key);
    else storage.setItem(key, value);
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

===== FILE: frontend/src/views/composeView.js =====
/**
 * Create / edit event form in a <dialog>, validated client-side against the lexicon.
 * @module views/composeView
 */
import { closeDialog, h, openDialog } from '../dom.js';
import { buildEventRecord, parseAtUri, validateEventRecord, validateHashtag } from '../records.js';

/**
 * ISO timestamp → value for <input type="datetime-local"> in local time.
 * @param {string} iso
 * @returns {string}
 */
export function isoToLocalInput(iso) {
  if (!iso) return '';
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const p = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}T${p(d.getHours())}:${p(d.getMinutes())}`;
}

/**
 * datetime-local value (local time) → ISO string, or "" if empty/invalid.
 * @param {string} value
 * @returns {string}
 */
export function localInputToIso(value) {
  if (!value) return '';
  const d = new Date(value);
  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? '' : d.toISOString();
}

const FIELDS = ['name', 'description', 'startsAt', 'endsAt', 'locationName', 'address', 'lat', 'lng', 'tags', 'group', 'hashtag'];

/**
 * Read raw values from the compose form.
 * @param {HTMLFormElement} form
 * @returns {Record<string, string>}
 */
export function readForm(form) {
  return Object.fromEntries(FIELDS.map((f) => [f, form.elements[f]?.value ?? '']));
}

/**
 * Turn form values into a record and validate it.
 * Error keys are form field names (coords errors are reported on "lat").
 * @param {Record<string, string>} values
 * @param {{createdAt?: string, sourceUrl?: string}} [base] preserved fields when editing
 * @returns {{record: object, errors: Record<string, string>}}
 */
export function validateForm(values, base = {}) {
  const record = buildEventRecord({
    ...values,
    startsAt: localInputToIso(values.startsAt),
    endsAt: localInputToIso(values.endsAt),
    createdAt: base.createdAt,
    sourceUrl: base.sourceUrl,
  });
  const errors = validateEventRecord(record);
  const partialCoords = Boolean(values.lat?.trim()) !== Boolean(values.lng?.trim());
  if (partialCoords) errors.coords = 'Enter both latitude and longitude, or neither.';
  if (errors.coords) {
    errors.lat = errors.coords;
    delete errors.coords;
  }
  const tagErr = validateHashtag(values.hashtag);
  if (tagErr) errors.hashtag = tagErr;
  return { record, errors };
}

function field(name, label, input, hint) {
  input.id = `c-${name}`;
  input.name = name;
  return h('div', { class: 'field' },
    h('label', { for: input.id }, label),
    hint && h('p', { class: 'hint', id: `c-${name}-hint` }, hint),
    input,
    h('p', { class: 'field__error', id: `c-${name}-err` }),
  );
}

/**
 * Show errors next to fields (aria-invalid + aria-describedby) and focus the first one.
 * @param {HTMLFormElement} form
 * @param {Record<string, string>} errors
 */
export function showErrors(form, errors) {
  for (const f of FIELDS) {
    const input = form.elements[f];
    const err = form.querySelector(`#c-${f}-err`);
    if (!input || !err) continue;
    const msg = errors[f] || '';
    err.textContent = msg;
    input.setAttribute('aria-invalid', String(Boolean(msg)));
    const hint = form.querySelector(`#c-${f}-hint`) ? `c-${f}-hint ` : '';
    input.setAttribute('aria-describedby', `${hint}c-${f}-err`.trim());
  }
  const first = FIELDS.find((f) => errors[f]);
  if (first) form.elements[first].focus();
}

/**
 * Build the compose form element (pre-filled when editing).
 * @param {object|null} ev EventView being edited, or null for a new event
 * @returns {HTMLFormElement}
 */
export function buildForm(ev) {
  const loc = ev?.location || {};
  const input = (attrs) => h('input', attrs);
  return h('form', { class: 'compose', novalidate: true },
    field('name', 'Event name', input({ required: true, maxlength: 200, value: ev?.name ?? '' })),
    field('description', 'Description', h('textarea', { rows: 4, maxlength: 3000, value: ev?.description ?? '' })),
    h('div', { class: 'field-row' },
      field('startsAt', 'Starts', input({ type: 'datetime-local', required: true, value: isoToLocalInput(ev?.startsAt) })),
      field('endsAt', 'Ends', input({ type: 'datetime-local', required: true, value: isoToLocalInput(ev?.endsAt) })),
    ),
    field('locationName', 'Location name', input({ required: true, maxlength: 200, value: loc.name ?? '', placeholder: 'Price Center East' })),
    field('address', 'Address (optional)', input({ maxlength: 300, value: loc.address ?? '' })),
    h('div', { class: 'field-row' },
      field('lat', 'Latitude (optional)', input({ inputmode: 'decimal', value: loc.lat ?? '' })),
      field('lng', 'Longitude (optional)', input({ inputmode: 'decimal', value: loc.lng ?? '' })),
    ),
    h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', dataset: { action: 'locate' } }, 'Use my location'),
    field('tags', 'Tags', input({ value: (ev?.tags || []).join(', '), placeholder: 'free food, music' }), 'Comma-separated, up to 10.'),
    field('group', 'Group / org (optional)', input({ maxlength: 100, value: ev?.group ?? '' })),
    field('hashtag', 'Sync from Bluesky hashtag (optional)', input({ maxlength: 64, placeholder: 'ucsdhackathon' }),
      'Your Bluesky posts with this hashtag appear as updates on the event.'),
    h('p', { class: 'form-status', role: 'status' }),
    h('div', { class: 'card__actions' },
      h('button', { type: 'submit', class: 'btn btn--primary' }, ev ? 'Save changes' : 'Create event'),
      ev && h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn btn--danger', dataset: { action: 'delete' } }, 'Delete'),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', dataset: { action: 'cancel' } }, 'Cancel'),
    ),
  );
}

/**
 * Create the compose dialog controller.
 * @param {{getWriter: () => object|null, geo?: {request(): Promise<{lat: number, lng: number}>},
 *   onSaved?: (r: {uri: string}) => void, onDeleted?: (uri: string) => void,
 *   toast?: (msg: string, o?: object) => void, confirmImpl?: (msg: string) => boolean}} deps
 * @returns {{open(ev?: object|null): void, remove(ev: object): Promise<void>, element: HTMLDialogElement}}
 */
export function createComposeView({ getWriter, geo, onSaved, onDeleted, toast = () => {}, confirmImpl = (m) => globalThis.confirm(m) }) {
  const dialog = h('dialog', { class: 'dialog', 'aria-labelledby': 'compose-title' });

  function open(ev = null) {
    const form = buildForm(ev);
    const status = form.querySelector('.form-status');
    form.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      submit(form, ev, status);
    });
    form.querySelector('[data-action=cancel]').onclick = () => closeDialog(dialog);
    form.querySelector('[data-action=locate]').onclick = () => locate(form, status);
    const del = form.querySelector('[data-action=delete]');
    if (del) del.onclick = () => remove(ev);
    dialog.replaceChildren(h('h2', { id: 'compose-title' }, ev ? 'Edit event' : 'New event'), form);
    openDialog(dialog);
  }

  async function locate(form, status) {
    try {
      const p = await geo.request();
      form.elements.lat.value = p.lat.toFixed(6);
      form.elements.lng.value = p.lng.toFixed(6);
    } catch (err) {
      status.textContent = err.message;
    }
  }

  async function submit(form, ev, status) {
    const values = readForm(form);
    const { record, errors } = validateForm(values, ev ? { createdAt: ev.createdAt ?? ev.indexedAt, sourceUrl: ev.sourceUrl } : {});
    showErrors(form, errors);
    if (Object.keys(errors).length) return;
    const writer = getWriter();
    if (!writer) return void (status.textContent = 'Log in to post events.');
    const btn = form.querySelector('[type=submit]');
    btn.disabled = true;
    status.textContent = 'Saving…';
    try {
      const res = ev ? await writer.updateEvent(parseAtUri(ev.uri).rkey, record) : await writer.createEvent(record);
      if (values.hashtag.trim()) await writer.createListener(res.uri, values.hashtag);
      closeDialog(dialog);
      onSaved?.(res);
    } catch (err) {
      status.textContent = `Could not save: ${err.message}`;
    } finally {
      btn.disabled = false;
    }
  }

  async function remove(ev) {
    const writer = getWriter();
    if (!writer || !confirmImpl(`Delete “${ev.name}”?`)) return;
    try {
      await writer.deleteEvent(parseAtUri(ev.uri).rkey);
      closeDialog(dialog);
      onDeleted?.(ev.uri);
    } catch (err) {
      toast(`Could not delete: ${err.message}`, { kind: 'error' });
    }
  }

  return { open, remove, element: dialog };
}

===== FILE: frontend/src/views/detailView.js =====
/**
 * Event detail <dialog>: full description and the announcement list from GET /api/event.
 * @module views/detailView
 */
import { closeDialog, h, openDialog, safeHttpUrl } from '../dom.js';
import { isUpdated } from '../filters.js';
import { formatDateTime, formatTimeWindow, osmUrl, statusBadges } from '../format.js';


/**
 * Render the dialog body for an event.
 * @param {object} ev EventView & {updates}
 * @param {{canEdit?: boolean, onEdit?: (ev: object) => void, onDelete?: (ev: object) => void,
 *   stale?: boolean, locale?: string, timeZone?: string}} [opts]
 * @returns {HTMLElement[]}
 */
export function renderDetail(ev, opts = {}) {
  const href = osmUrl(ev);
  const source = safeHttpUrl(ev.sourceUrl);
  const badges = statusBadges(ev.status);
  const updates = [...(ev.updates || [])].sort((a, b) => Date.parse(b.createdAt) - Date.parse(a.createdAt));
  return [
    h('h2', { id: 'detail-title' }, ev.name),
    h('div', { class: 'card__badges' },
      ...badges.map((b) => h('span', { class: `badge badge--${b.mod}` }, b.label)),
      isUpdated(ev) && h('span', { class: 'badge badge--updated' }, 'Updated'),
      opts.stale && h('span', { class: 'badge' }, 'Saved copy'),
    ),
    ev.status === 'ended' && h('p', { class: 'notice notice--ended', role: 'status' }, 'This event has ended.'),
    h('p', null, h('time', { datetime: ev.startsAt }, formatTimeWindow(ev.startsAt, ev.endsAt, opts))),
    h('p', null,
      href ? h('a', { href, target: '_blank', rel: 'noopener noreferrer' }, ev.location?.name) : ev.location?.name,
      ev.location?.address && h('span', { class: 'muted' }, ` — ${ev.location.address}`),
    ),
    ev.group && h('p', { class: 'card__group' }, ev.group),
    ev.authorHandle && h('p', { class: 'muted' }, `Posted by @${ev.authorHandle}`),
    ev.description && h('p', { class: 'detail__desc' }, ev.description),
    source && h('p', null, h('a', { href: source, target: '_blank', rel: 'noopener noreferrer' }, 'Event page')),
    h('section', { 'aria-labelledby': 'detail-updates' },
      h('h3', { id: 'detail-updates' }, 'Updates'),
      updates.length
        ? h('ol', { class: 'updates' }, updates.map((u) => h('li', null,
            h('time', { datetime: u.createdAt, class: 'muted' }, formatDateTime(u.createdAt, opts)),
            h('p', null, u.text),
          )))
        : h('p', { class: 'muted' }, 'No announcements yet.'),
    ),
    opts.canEdit && h('div', { class: 'card__actions' },
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', onclick: () => opts.onEdit?.(ev) }, 'Edit'),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn btn--danger', onclick: () => opts.onDelete?.(ev) }, 'Delete'),
    ),
  ];
}

/**
 * Create the detail dialog controller.
 * @param {{api: {getEvent(uri: string): Promise<{data: object, stale: boolean}>},
 *   canEdit?: (ev: object) => boolean, onEdit?: (ev: object) => void, onDelete?: (ev: object) => void}} deps
 * @returns {{open(uri: string): Promise<void>, close(): void, element: HTMLDialogElement}}
 */
export function createDetailView({ api, canEdit = () => false, onEdit, onDelete, onOpenUri, onClose }) {
  const body = h('div', { class: 'dialog__body' });
  const close = () => {
    closeDialog(dialog);
    onClose?.();
  };
  const dialog = h('dialog', { class: 'dialog', 'aria-labelledby': 'detail-title' },
    h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn dialog__close', 'aria-label': 'Close', onclick: close }, '×'),
    body,
  );
  dialog.addEventListener('close', () => onClose?.());

  async function open(uri) {
    onOpenUri?.(uri);
    body.replaceChildren(h('p', { id: 'detail-title', role: 'status' }, 'Loading…'));
    openDialog(dialog);
    try {
      const { data, stale } = await api.getEvent(uri);
      body.replaceChildren(...renderDetail(data, {
        stale,
        canEdit: canEdit(data),
        onEdit: (ev) => { close(); onEdit?.(ev); },
        onDelete: (ev) => { close(); onDelete?.(ev); },
      }).filter(Boolean));
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err?.status === 404 ? 'This event is no longer available.' : 'Could not load this event.';
      body.replaceChildren(h('p', { id: 'detail-title', role: 'alert' }, msg));
    }
  }

  return { open, close, element: dialog };
}
===== FILE: frontend/src/views/filterBar.js =====
/**
 * Filter bar: debounced text search, group select, tag chips, "Near me" toggle.
 * Stateless UI: it renders a FilterState and reports changes via onChange(patch).
 * @module views/filterBar
 */
import { h } from '../dom.js';
import { toggleTag } from '../filters.js';
import { debounce } from '../util.js';

/**
 * @param {HTMLElement} root
 * @param {{onChange: (patch: Partial<import('../filters.js').FilterState>) => void,
 *   debounceMs?: number, geoSupported?: boolean}} opts
 * @returns {{render(state: import('../filters.js').FilterState,
 *   options: {tags: {value: string, count: number}[], groups: {value: string, count: number}[],
 *   nearMeBusy?: boolean}): void}}
 */
export function createFilterBar(root, { onChange, debounceMs = 300, geoSupported = true }) {
  let state = { tags: [], group: '', q: '', nearMe: false, sort: 'soonest' };
  const emitQ = debounce((q) => onChange({ q }), debounceMs);

  const search = h('input', {
    type: 'search', id: 'f-q', name: 'q', autocomplete: 'off', placeholder: 'Search events',
    oninput: (e) => emitQ(e.target.value),
  });
  const group = h('select', { id: 'f-group', name: 'group', onchange: (e) => onChange({ group: e.target.value }) });
  const sort = h('select', { id: 'f-sort', name: 'sort', onchange: (e) => onChange({ sort: e.target.value }) },
    h('option', { value: 'soonest' }, 'Soonest'),
    h('option', { value: 'newest' }, 'Newest'),
  );
  const nearMe = h('button', {
    type: 'button', class: 'btn', 'aria-pressed': 'false', hidden: !geoSupported,
    onclick: () => onChange({ nearMe: !state.nearMe }),
  }, 'Near me');
  const chips = h('ul', { class: 'chips', 'aria-label': 'Filter by tag' });

  root.replaceChildren(
    h('div', { class: 'filters__row' },
      h('label', { for: 'f-q', class: 'visually-hidden' }, 'Search events'), search,
      h('label', { for: 'f-group', class: 'visually-hidden' }, 'Group'), group,
      h('label', { for: 'f-sort', class: 'visually-hidden' }, 'Sort events'), sort,
      nearMe,
    ),
    chips,
  );

  function render(next, { tags = [], groups = [], nearMeBusy = false } = {}) {
    state = next;
    if (document.activeElement !== search && search.value !== next.q) search.value = next.q;
    renderGroups(groups, next.group);
    sort.value = next.sort || 'soonest';
    nearMe.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(next.nearMe));
    nearMe.textContent = nearMeBusy ? 'Locating…' : 'Near me';
    renderChips(tags, next.tags);
  }

  function renderGroups(groups, selected) {
    const opts = [h('option', { value: '' }, 'All groups'),
      ...groups.map((g) => h('option', { value: g.value }, `${g.value} (${g.count})`))];
    if (selected && !groups.some((g) => g.value === selected)) opts.push(h('option', { value: selected }, selected));
    group.replaceChildren(...opts);
    group.value = selected || '';
  }

  function renderChips(tags, active) {
    const focused = document.activeElement?.dataset?.tag;
    const values = [...new Set([...active, ...tags.map((t) => t.value)])];
    chips.replaceChildren(...values.map((v) => h('li', null,
      h('button', {
        type: 'button', class: 'chip', 'aria-pressed': String(active.includes(v)), dataset: { tag: v },
        onclick: () => onChange({ tags: toggleTag(state.tags, v) }),
      }, `#${v}`),
    )));
    if (focused) [...chips.querySelectorAll('button')].find((b) => b.dataset.tag === focused)?.focus();
  }

  return { render };
}

===== FILE: frontend/src/views/listView.js =====
/**
 * Primary (accessible) list view: event cards grouped by day, with "Load more"
 * + IntersectionObserver infinite scroll, and empty / loading / offline states.
 * @module views/listView
 */
import { h } from '../dom.js';
import { groupByDay, isUpdated } from '../filters.js';
import { formatDistance, formatTimeWindow, osmUrl, statusLabel, statusBadges } from '../format.js';

/**
 * @typedef {{onOpen?: (uri: string) => void, onTagClick?: (tag: string) => void,
 *   onToggleSubscribe?: (uri: string) => void, activeTags?: string[],
 *   subscribed?: Set<string>, locale?: string, timeZone?: string}} CardOpts
 */

/**
 * Render one event card.
 * @param {object} ev EventView
 * @param {CardOpts} [opts]
 * @returns {HTMLElement}
 */
export function renderCard(ev, opts = {}) {
  const { onOpen, onTagClick, onToggleSubscribe, activeTags = [], subscribed = new Set() } = opts;
  const titleId = `ev-${hashId(ev.uri)}`;
  const badges = statusBadges(ev.status);
  const href = osmUrl(ev);
  const isSub = subscribed.has(ev.uri);
  const distance = formatDistance(ev.distanceKm);

  return h('article', { class: 'card', 'aria-labelledby': titleId, dataset: { uri: ev.uri } },
    h('div', { class: 'card__badges' },
      ...badges.map((b) => h('span', { class: `badge badge--${b.mod}` }, b.label)),
      isUpdated(ev) && h('span', { class: 'badge badge--updated' }, 'Updated'),
    ),
    h('h3', { class: 'card__title', id: titleId },
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'linklike', dataset: { focusKey: `open:${ev.uri}` }, onclick: () => onOpen?.(ev.uri) }, ev.name),
    ),
    h('p', { class: 'card__time' },
      h('time', { datetime: ev.startsAt }, formatTimeWindow(ev.startsAt, ev.endsAt, opts)),
    ),
    h('p', { class: 'card__loc' },
      href
        ? h('a', { href, target: '_blank', rel: 'noopener noreferrer' }, ev.location?.name || 'Map')
        : ev.location?.name,
      distance && h('span', { class: 'card__dist' }, ` · ${distance}`),
    ),
    ev.group && h('p', { class: 'card__group' }, ev.group),
    ev.tags?.length && h('ul', { class: 'chips', 'aria-label': 'Tags' },
      ev.tags.map((t) => h('li', null,
        h('button', {
          type: 'button', class: 'chip', 'aria-pressed': String(activeTags.includes(t)),
          'aria-label': `Filter by tag ${t}`, dataset: { focusKey: `tag:${ev.uri}:${t}` },
          onclick: () => onTagClick?.(t),
        }, `#${t}`),
      )),
    ),
    h('div', { class: 'card__actions' },
      h('button', {
        type: 'button', class: `btn ${isSub ? 'btn--on' : ''}`, 'aria-pressed': String(isSub),
        dataset: { focusKey: `sub:${ev.uri}` }, onclick: () => onToggleSubscribe?.(ev.uri),
      }, isSub ? 'Subscribed' : 'Subscribe'),
    ),
  );
}

function hashId(s) {
  let x = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) x = (x * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) | 0;
  return (x >>> 0).toString(36);
}

/**
 * @typedef {{events: object[], hasMore?: boolean, loading?: boolean, stale?: boolean,
 *   savedAt?: number, error?: string|null, now?: Date} & CardOpts} ListState
 */

/**
 * Create the list view.
 * @param {HTMLElement} root
 * @param {{onLoadMore?: () => void, IntersectionObserverImpl?: typeof IntersectionObserver|null}} [opts]
 * @returns {{render(state: ListState): void, destroy(): void}}
 */
export function createListView(root, { onLoadMore, IntersectionObserverImpl = globalThis.IntersectionObserver ?? null } = {}) {
  let loading = false;
  let hasMore = false;
  const more = () => {
    if (!loading && hasMore) onLoadMore?.();
  };
  const observer = IntersectionObserverImpl
    ? new IntersectionObserverImpl((entries) => entries.some((e) => e.isIntersecting) && more(), { rootMargin: '400px' })
    : null;

  function render(state) {
    loading = Boolean(state.loading);
    hasMore = Boolean(state.hasMore);
    const focusKey = document.activeElement?.dataset?.focusKey;
    observer?.disconnect();
    root.setAttribute('aria-busy', String(loading));
    root.replaceChildren(...content(state));
    if (focusKey) [...root.querySelectorAll('[data-focus-key]')].find((el) => el.dataset.focusKey === focusKey)?.focus();
    const sentinel = root.querySelector('.load-more');
    if (sentinel && observer) observer.observe(sentinel);
  }

  function content(state) {
    const nodes = [];
    if (state.stale) nodes.push(staleBanner(state.savedAt));
    if (state.error) nodes.push(h('p', { class: 'notice notice--error', role: 'alert' }, state.error));

    const events = state.events || [];
    if (!events.length) {
      if (!state.loading && !state.error) {
        nodes.push(h('p', { class: 'empty' }, 'No events match your filters. Try clearing a tag or search.'));
      }
    } else if (state.sort === 'newest') {
      nodes.push(h('section', { class: 'day', 'aria-labelledby': 'sort-newest' },
        h('h2', { class: 'day__title', id: 'sort-newest' }, 'Newest'),
        h('div', { class: 'grid' }, events.map((ev) => renderCard(ev, state))),
      ));
    } else {
      for (const g of groupByDay(events, { now: state.now, locale: state.locale })) {
        const id = `day-${g.key}`;
        nodes.push(h('section', { class: 'day', 'aria-labelledby': id },
          h('h2', { class: 'day__title', id }, g.label),
          h('div', { class: 'grid' }, g.events.map((ev) => renderCard(ev, state))),
        ));
      }
    }

    if (state.loading) nodes.push(h('p', { class: 'loading', role: 'status' }, 'Loading events…'));
    if (state.hasMore) {
      nodes.push(h('button', {
        type: 'button', class: 'btn load-more', disabled: loading, dataset: { focusKey: 'load-more' }, onclick: more,
      }, loading ? 'Loading…' : 'Load more'));
    }
    return nodes;
  }

  function staleBanner(savedAt) {
    const when = savedAt ? ` from ${new Date(savedAt).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' })}` : '';
    return h('p', { class: 'notice notice--stale', role: 'status' },
      `You're offline or the connection is poor — showing saved events${when}.`);
  }

  return { render, destroy: () => observer?.disconnect() };
}

===== FILE: frontend/src/views/loginView.js =====
/**
 * Login dialog (Bluesky handle + app password).
 * @module views/loginView
 */
import { closeDialog, h, openDialog } from '../dom.js';

/**
 * @param {{login: (identifier: string, password: string) => Promise<any>, onLoggedIn?: () => void}} deps
 * @returns {{open(): void, element: HTMLDialogElement}}
 */
export function createLoginView({ login, onLoggedIn }) {
  const dialog = h('dialog', { class: 'dialog', 'aria-labelledby': 'login-title' });

  function open() {
    const status = h('p', { class: 'form-status', role: 'status' });
    const submit = h('button', { type: 'submit', class: 'btn btn--primary' }, 'Log in');
    const form = h('form', { class: 'compose' },
      h('div', { class: 'field' },
        h('label', { for: 'l-id' }, 'Bluesky handle'),
        h('input', { id: 'l-id', name: 'identifier', required: true, autocomplete: 'username', autocapitalize: 'none', placeholder: 'you.bsky.social' }),
      ),
      h('div', { class: 'field' },
        h('label', { for: 'l-pw' }, 'App password'),
        h('p', { class: 'hint', id: 'l-pw-hint' }, 'Create one in Bluesky → Settings → Privacy and security → App passwords. Never use your main password.'),
        h('input', { id: 'l-pw', name: 'password', type: 'password', required: true, autocomplete: 'current-password', 'aria-describedby': 'l-pw-hint' }),
      ),
      status,
      h('div', { class: 'card__actions' }, submit,
        h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', onclick: () => closeDialog(dialog) }, 'Cancel')),
    );
    form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      submit.disabled = true;
      status.textContent = 'Logging in…';
      try {
        await login(form.elements.identifier.value, form.elements.password.value);
        closeDialog(dialog);
        onLoggedIn?.();
      } catch (err) {
        status.textContent = `Login failed: ${err.message}`;
      } finally {
        submit.disabled = false;
      }
    });
    dialog.replaceChildren(h('h2', { id: 'login-title' }, 'Log in with Bluesky'), form);
    openDialog(dialog);
  }

  return { open, element: dialog };
}

===== FILE: frontend/src/views/mapView.js =====
/**
 * Secondary map tab. Leaflet is loaded lazily (only when the tab first opens).
 * The tile provider is configuration so it can be swapped (e.g. Overture/Protomaps).
 * @module views/mapView
 */
import { h } from '../dom.js';
import { coordsOf } from '../filters.js';
import { formatTimeWindow } from '../format.js';

/** Default map configuration. Override any field via createMapView({config}). */
export const MAP_CONFIG = Object.freeze({
  leafletJs: 'https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.js',
  leafletCss: 'https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.css',
  tileUrl: 'https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png',
  attribution: '&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a> contributors',
  maxZoom: 19,
  center: [32.8801, -117.234], // UCSD
  zoom: 15,
});

/**
 * Pure: events → marker descriptors (only events with valid coordinates).
 * @param {object[]} events
 * @param {{locale?: string, timeZone?: string}} [opts]
 * @returns {{uri: string, lat: number, lng: number, title: string, subtitle: string}[]}
 */
export function buildMarkers(events, opts = {}) {
  return (events || []).flatMap((ev) => {
    const c = coordsOf(ev);
    if (!c) return [];
    const when = formatTimeWindow(ev.startsAt, ev.endsAt, opts);
    return [{ uri: ev.uri, lat: c.lat, lng: c.lng, title: ev.name, subtitle: `${when} · ${ev.location?.name ?? ''}` }];
  });
}

let leafletPromise = null;

/**
 * Inject Leaflet's CSS + script once and resolve with window.L.
 * @param {typeof MAP_CONFIG} config
 * @returns {Promise<any>}
 */
export function loadLeaflet(config = MAP_CONFIG) {
  if (globalThis.L) return Promise.resolve(globalThis.L);
  leafletPromise ??= new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    document.head.append(h('link', { rel: 'stylesheet', href: config.leafletCss }));
    const script = h('script', { src: config.leafletJs, async: true });
    script.onload = () => resolve(globalThis.L);
    script.onerror = () => {
      leafletPromise = null;
      script.remove();
      reject(new Error('Map library failed to load'));
    };
    document.head.append(script);
  });
  return leafletPromise;
}

/**
 * Create the map view.
 * @param {HTMLElement} root
 * @param {{onOpen?: (uri: string) => void, config?: Partial<typeof MAP_CONFIG>,
 *   load?: (c: typeof MAP_CONFIG) => Promise<any>}} [opts]
 * @returns {{show(events: object[]): Promise<void>}}
 */
export function createMapView(root, { onOpen, config = {}, load = loadLeaflet } = {}) {
  const cfg = { ...MAP_CONFIG, ...config };
  let map = null;
  let layer = null;

  async function ensureMap() {
    if (map) return map;
    root.replaceChildren(h('p', { role: 'status' }, 'Loading map…'));
    const L = await load(cfg);
    const el = h('div', { class: 'map', role: 'region', 'aria-label': 'Event map' });
    root.replaceChildren(el, h('p', { class: 'muted' }, 'The list view has every event and is fully keyboard accessible.'));
    map = L.map(el).setView(cfg.center, cfg.zoom);
    L.tileLayer(cfg.tileUrl, { maxZoom: cfg.maxZoom, attribution: cfg.attribution }).addTo(map);
    layer = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
    return map;
  }

  function popup(m) {
    return h('div', null,
      h('strong', null, m.title), h('br'), h('span', null, m.subtitle), h('br'),
      h('button', { type: 'button', class: 'btn', onclick: () => onOpen?.(m.uri) }, 'Details'),
    );
  }

  async function show(events) {
    try {
      await ensureMap();
    } catch {
      root.replaceChildren(h('p', { class: 'notice notice--error', role: 'alert' },
        'The map could not load (poor connection?). The list view still works.'));
      return;
    }
    const L = globalThis.L;
    layer.clearLayers();
    const markers = buildMarkers(events);
    for (const m of markers) L.marker([m.lat, m.lng], { title: m.title }).bindPopup(popup(m)).addTo(layer);
    map.invalidateSize();
    if (markers.length) map.fitBounds(markers.map((m) => [m.lat, m.lng]), { maxZoom: 17, padding: [24, 24] });
  }

  return { show };
}

===== FILE: frontend/sw.js =====
/* Community Map service worker (classic script).
 * - App shell: cache-first.
 * - GET /api/*: network-first, falling back to the cached copy (marked with x-cm-stale: 1).
 * - Never cached: non-GET requests and the SSE notification stream.
 */
const VERSION = 'v2';
const SHELL_CACHE = `cm-shell-${VERSION}`;
const API_CACHE = `cm-api-${VERSION}`;
const SHELL = [
  './', './index.html', './styles.css', './manifest.webmanifest', './icon.svg',
  './src/app.js', './src/api.js', './src/atproto.js', './src/cache.js', './src/devWriter.js',
  './src/dom.js', './src/filters.js', './src/format.js', './src/geo.js', './src/records.js',
  './src/store.js', './src/subscriptions.js', './src/toast.js', './src/util.js',
  './src/views/composeView.js', './src/views/detailView.js', './src/views/filterBar.js',
  './src/views/listView.js', './src/views/loginView.js', './src/views/mapView.js',
];

self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(caches.open(SHELL_CACHE).then((c) => c.addAll(SHELL)).then(() => self.skipWaiting()));
});

self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  const keep = new Set([SHELL_CACHE, API_CACHE]);
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.keys()
      .then((keys) => Promise.all(keys.filter((k) => k.startsWith('cm-') && !keep.has(k)).map((k) => caches.delete(k))))
      .then(() => self.clients.claim()),
  );
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', (event) => {
  const req = event.request;
  if (req.method !== 'GET') return;
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  if (url.origin !== self.location.origin) return; // Leaflet/tiles: browser default
  const isApi = url.pathname.includes('/api/');
  if (isApi && (url.pathname.endsWith('/api/notifications/stream') || (req.headers.get('accept') || '').includes('text/event-stream'))) return;
  event.respondWith(isApi ? networkFirst(req) : cacheFirst(req));
});

async function networkFirst(req) {
  const cache = await caches.open(API_CACHE);
  try {
    const res = await fetch(req);
    if (res.ok) cache.put(req, res.clone());
    return res;
  } catch (err) {
    const hit = await cache.match(req);
    if (!hit) throw err; // let the page's own cache fallback handle it
    const headers = new Headers(hit.headers);
    headers.set('x-cm-stale', '1');
    return new Response(await hit.blob(), { status: hit.status, statusText: hit.statusText, headers });
  }
}

async function cacheFirst(req) {
  const hit = await caches.match(req, { ignoreSearch: true });
  if (hit) return hit;
  const res = await fetch(req);
  if (res.ok) (await caches.open(SHELL_CACHE)).put(req, res.clone());
  return res;
}

===== FILE: frontend/tests/api.test.js =====
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ApiError, createApi } from '../src/api.js';
import { createCache } from '../src/cache.js';
import { jsonResponse, memoryStorage } from './helpers.js';

const mk = (fetchImpl, extra = {}) => createApi({ fetchImpl, cache: createCache({ storage: memoryStorage(), now: () => 1000 }), ...extra });

describe('api', () => {
  it('builds GET URLs with baseUrl and repeated tags', async () => {
    const f = vi.fn(async () => jsonResponse({ events: [], cursor: null }));
    const api = mk(f, { baseUrl: 'https://x.test/' });
    const r = await api.listEvents({ tag: ['a', 'b'], q: 'hi', limit: 10 });
    expect(f.mock.calls[0][0]).toBe('https://x.test/api/events?tag=a&tag=b&q=hi&limit=10');
    expect(f.mock.calls[0][1].method).toBe('GET');
    expect(r).toEqual({ data: { events: [], cursor: null }, stale: false });
  });

  it('wraps every endpoint path from CONTRACTS.md', async () => {
    const f = vi.fn(async () => jsonResponse([]));
    const api = mk(f);
    await api.health(); await api.config(); await api.getEvent('at://x/y/z'); await api.tags(); await api.groups();
    await api.listSubscriptions('s1'); await api.notifications('s1', 7);
    const urls = f.mock.calls.map((c) => c[0]);
    expect(urls).toEqual([
      '/api/health', '/api/config', '/api/event?uri=at%3A%2F%2Fx%2Fy%2Fz', '/api/tags', '/api/groups',
      '/api/subscriptions?subscriberId=s1', '/api/notifications?subscriberId=s1&since=7',
    ]);
    expect(api.notificationStreamUrl('s 1')).toBe('/api/notifications/stream?subscriberId=s+1');
  });

  it('sends JSON bodies for writes and handles 204', async () => {
    const f = vi.fn(async () => jsonResponse(undefined, 204));
    const api = mk(f);
    expect(await api.subscribe('s1', 'at://e')).toBeNull();
    await api.unsubscribe('s1', 'at://e');
    await api.importIcal('https://cal.test/x.ics');
    await api.postDevRecord({ did: 'd' });
    const calls = f.mock.calls.map(([u, i]) => [i.method, u, JSON.parse(i.body)]);
    expect(calls).toEqual([
      ['POST', '/api/subscriptions', { subscriberId: 's1', eventUri: 'at://e' }],
      ['DELETE', '/api/subscriptions', { subscriberId: 's1', eventUri: 'at://e' }],
      ['POST', '/api/import/ical', { url: 'https://cal.test/x.ics' }],
      ['POST', '/api/dev/records', { did: 'd' }],
    ]);
    expect(f.mock.calls[0][1].headers['Content-Type']).toBe('application/json');
  });

  it('falls back to the cached response on network failure, marked stale', async () => {
    let online = true;
    const f = vi.fn(async () => {
      if (!online) throw new TypeError('Failed to fetch');
      return jsonResponse([{ value: 'food', count: 3 }]);
    });
    const api = mk(f);
    await api.tags();
    online = false;
    expect(await api.tags()).toEqual({ data: [{ value: 'food', count: 3 }], stale: true, savedAt: 1000 });
  });

  it('falls back on 5xx but throws on 4xx', async () => {
    const responses = [jsonResponse({ uri: 'u' }), jsonResponse({ detail: 'x' }, 503), jsonResponse({ detail: 'nf' }, 404)];
    const api = mk(vi.fn(async () => responses.shift()));
    await api.getEvent('u');
    expect((await api.getEvent('u')).stale).toBe(true);
    await expect(api.getEvent('u')).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404, body: { detail: 'nf' } });
  });

  it('throws an offline ApiError when nothing is cached', async () => {
    const api = mk(vi.fn(async () => { throw new TypeError('offline'); }));
    const err = await api.config().catch((e) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(err.offline).toBe(true);
  });

  it('times out via AbortController and falls back', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const ok = vi.fn(async () => jsonResponse({ devMode: true }));
    const cache = createCache({ storage: memoryStorage() });
    await createApi({ fetchImpl: ok, cache }).config();
    const hang = (url, init) => new Promise((_, rej) => init.signal.addEventListener('abort', () => rej(new DOMException('aborted', 'AbortError'))));
    const p = createApi({ fetchImpl: hang, cache, timeoutMs: 50 }).config();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(60);
    expect(await p).toMatchObject({ data: { devMode: true }, stale: true });
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('marks service-worker cache hits stale and does not re-cache them', async () => {
    const api = mk(vi.fn(async () => jsonResponse({ ok: true }, 200, { 'x-cm-stale': '1' })));
    expect(await api.config()).toEqual({ data: { ok: true }, stale: true });
  });

  it('does not serve notifications from cache', async () => {
    let n = 0;
    const api = mk(vi.fn(async () => { if (n++) throw new TypeError('off'); return jsonResponse([{ id: 1 }]); }));
    await api.notifications('s', null);
    await expect(api.notifications('s', null)).rejects.toMatchObject({ offline: true });
  });

  it('writes do not fall back and report offline', async () => {
    const api = mk(vi.fn(async () => { throw new TypeError('off'); }));
    await expect(api.subscribe('s', 'u')).rejects.toMatchObject({ offline: true });
  });
});

===== FILE: frontend/tests/app.test.js =====
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { jsonResponse, memoryStorage, sampleEvent } from './helpers.js';

globalThis.__CM_NO_AUTOSTART__ = true;
const { start } = await import('../src/app.js');

const html = readFileSync(resolve(__dirname, '../index.html'), 'utf8');
const flush = () => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));

function routes(url) {
  const path = url.split('?')[0];
  return {
    '/api/config': { devMode: true, jetstreamEnabled: false, eventNsid: 'app.communitymap.event', listenerNsid: 'app.communitymap.listener' },
    '/api/events': { events: [sampleEvent({ startsAt: new Date(Date.now() + 3600e3).toISOString(), endsAt: new Date(Date.now() + 7200e3).toISOString() })], cursor: null },
    '/api/tags': [{ value: 'food', count: 1 }],
    '/api/groups': [{ value: 'Triton Eats', count: 1 }],
    '/api/subscriptions': [],
  }[path];
}

describe('app (smoke)', () => {
  afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals());

  it('boots in demo mode and renders events, tags and the New event button', async () => {
    document.documentElement.innerHTML = html.replace(/<script[^>]*><\/script>/, '');
    vi.stubGlobal('localStorage', memoryStorage());
    vi.stubGlobal('sessionStorage', memoryStorage());
    const f = vi.fn(async (url) => jsonResponse(routes(url) ?? { error: 'nf' }, routes(url) ? 200 : 404));
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', f);
    await start();
    await flush(); await flush();
    expect(document.querySelectorAll('#list article')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(document.querySelector('#filters .chip').textContent).toBe('#food');
    expect(document.querySelector('#account').textContent).toContain('Demo mode');
    expect(document.querySelector('#account').textContent).toContain('New event');
    // Location is never sent to the backend.
    expect(f.mock.calls.every(([u]) => !/lat|lng|near/i.test(u))).toBe(true);

    document.querySelector('#filters .chip').click();
    await flush();
    expect(f.mock.calls.some(([u]) => u.startsWith('/api/events?') && u.includes('tag=food'))).toBe(true);

    document.querySelector('#tab-map').click();
    expect(document.querySelector('#panel-map').hidden).toBe(false);
    expect(document.querySelector('#panel-list').hidden).toBe(true);
  });
});

===== FILE: frontend/tests/atproto.test.js =====
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createAtprotoWriter, didDocUrl, pdsFromDidDoc } from '../src/atproto.js';
import { buildEventRecord } from '../src/records.js';
import { jsonResponse, memoryStorage } from './helpers.js';

const DID = 'did:plc:alice123';
const PDS = 'https://pds.example.com';
const didDoc = { id: DID, service: [{ id: '#atproto_pds', type: 'AtprotoPersonalDataServer', serviceEndpoint: `${PDS}/` }] };

function fakeNetwork(overrides = {}) {
  const routes = {
    'https://public.api.bsky.app/xrpc/com.atproto.identity.resolveHandle?handle=alice.test': () => jsonResponse({ did: DID }),
    [`https://plc.directory/${DID}`]: () => jsonResponse(didDoc),
    [`${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.server.createSession`]: () => jsonResponse({ did: DID, handle: 'alice.test', accessJwt: 'A1', refreshJwt: 'R1' }),
    [`${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.repo.createRecord`]: () => jsonResponse({ uri: `at://${DID}/app.communitymap.event/3kxyz`, cid: 'bafyc' }),
    [`${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.repo.putRecord`]: () => jsonResponse({ uri: 'u', cid: 'c' }),
    [`${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.repo.deleteRecord`]: () => jsonResponse({}),
    ...overrides,
  };
  return vi.fn(async (url) => {
    const r = routes[url];
    if (!r) return jsonResponse({ error: 'NotFound' }, 404);
    return r();
  });
}

const body = (call) => JSON.parse(call[1].body);

describe('atproto helpers', () => {
  it('resolves DID document URLs for plc and web', () => {
    expect(didDocUrl('did:plc:x')).toBe('https://plc.directory/did:plc:x');
    expect(didDocUrl('did:web:ucsd.edu')).toBe('https://ucsd.edu/.well-known/did.json');
    expect(didDocUrl('did:web:localhost%3A3000:u:bob')).toBe('https://localhost:3000/u/bob/did.json');
    expect(() => didDocUrl('did:key:z')).toThrow();
  });

  it('finds the PDS service endpoint', () => {
    expect(pdsFromDidDoc(didDoc)).toBe(PDS);
    expect(pdsFromDidDoc({ service: [] })).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('atproto writer', () => {
  it('login: resolveHandle → DID doc → createSession, persisted to sessionStorage', async () => {
    const f = fakeNetwork();
    const storage = memoryStorage();
    const w = createAtprotoWriter({ fetchImpl: f, storage });
    expect(await w.login('@alice.test', 'app-pw')).toEqual({ did: DID, handle: 'alice.test' });
    expect(f.mock.calls.map((c) => c[0])).toEqual([
      'https://public.api.bsky.app/xrpc/com.atproto.identity.resolveHandle?handle=alice.test',
      `https://plc.directory/${DID}`,
      `${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.server.createSession`,
    ]);
    expect(body(f.mock.calls[2])).toEqual({ identifier: 'alice.test', password: 'app-pw' });
    expect(w.did).toBe(DID);
    // restored by a new instance from the same sessionStorage
    expect(createAtprotoWriter({ fetchImpl: f, storage }).loggedIn).toBe(true);
  });

  it('falls back to the second resolver', async () => {
    const f = fakeNetwork({
      'https://public.api.bsky.app/xrpc/com.atproto.identity.resolveHandle?handle=alice.test': () => { throw new TypeError('net'); },
      'https://bsky.social/xrpc/com.atproto.identity.resolveHandle?handle=alice.test': () => jsonResponse({ did: DID }),
    });
    const w = createAtprotoWriter({ fetchImpl: f, storage: memoryStorage() });
    await w.login('alice.test', 'pw');
    expect(w.did).toBe(DID);
  });

  it('createEvent sends a lexicon-exact createRecord payload with bearer auth', async () => {
    const f = fakeNetwork();
    const w = createAtprotoWriter({ fetchImpl: f, storage: memoryStorage() });
    await w.login('alice.test', 'pw');
    const record = buildEventRecord({ name: 'Jam', startsAt: '2026-11-12T18:00:00.000Z', endsAt: '2026-11-12T20:00:00.000Z', locationName: 'Geisel', lat: 32.88, lng: -117.23, tags: 'study' });
    const res = await w.createEvent(record);
    expect(res.uri).toBe(`at://${DID}/app.communitymap.event/3kxyz`);
    const call = f.mock.calls.at(-1);
    expect(call[1].headers.Authorization).toBe('Bearer A1');
    expect(body(call)).toEqual({
      repo: DID,
      collection: 'app.communitymap.event',
      record: {
        $type: 'app.communitymap.event', name: 'Jam', startsAt: '2026-11-12T18:00:00.000Z', endsAt: '2026-11-12T20:00:00.000Z',
        location: { name: 'Geisel', lat: '32.88', lng: '-117.23' }, tags: ['study'], createdAt: record.createdAt,
      },
    });
  });

  it('updateEvent uses putRecord with rkey; deleteEvent uses deleteRecord; createListener', async () => {
    const f = fakeNetwork();
    const w = createAtprotoWriter({ fetchImpl: f, storage: memoryStorage() });
    await w.login('alice.test', 'pw');
    await w.updateEvent('3kxyz', { name: 'n' });
    expect(body(f.mock.calls.at(-1))).toMatchObject({ repo: DID, collection: 'app.communitymap.event', rkey: '3kxyz', record: { $type: 'app.communitymap.event', name: 'n' } });
    expect(await w.deleteEvent('3kxyz')).toEqual({ uri: `at://${DID}/app.communitymap.event/3kxyz` });
    expect(body(f.mock.calls.at(-1))).toEqual({ repo: DID, collection: 'app.communitymap.event', rkey: '3kxyz' });
    await w.createListener(`at://${DID}/app.communitymap.event/3kxyz`, '#StudyJam');
    const l = body(f.mock.calls.at(-1));
    expect(l.collection).toBe('app.communitymap.listener');
    expect(l.record).toEqual({ $type: 'app.communitymap.listener', subject: `at://${DID}/app.communitymap.event/3kxyz`, hashtag: 'studyjam', createdAt: l.record.createdAt });
  });

  it('refreshes the session on ExpiredToken and retries once', async () => {
    let first = true;
    const f = fakeNetwork({
      [`${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.repo.createRecord`]: () => {
        if (first) { first = false; return jsonResponse({ error: 'ExpiredToken', message: 'Token has expired' }, 401); }
        return jsonResponse({ uri: 'u2', cid: 'c2' });
      },
      [`${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.server.refreshSession`]: () => jsonResponse({ did: DID, handle: 'alice.test', accessJwt: 'A2', refreshJwt: 'R2' }),
    });
    const w = createAtprotoWriter({ fetchImpl: f, storage: memoryStorage() });
    await w.login('alice.test', 'pw');
    expect(await w.createEvent({ name: 'x' })).toEqual({ uri: 'u2', cid: 'c2' });
    const urls = f.mock.calls.map((c) => c[0]).slice(3);
    expect(urls).toEqual([`${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.repo.createRecord`, `${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.server.refreshSession`, `${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.repo.createRecord`]);
    expect(f.mock.calls[4][1].headers.Authorization).toBe('Bearer R1');
    expect(f.mock.calls[5][1].headers.Authorization).toBe('Bearer A2');
  });

  it('surfaces login errors and supports logout', async () => {
    const f = fakeNetwork({ [`${PDS}/xrpc/com.atproto.server.createSession`]: () => jsonResponse({ error: 'AuthenticationRequired', message: 'Invalid identifier or password' }, 401) });
    const w = createAtprotoWriter({ fetchImpl: f, storage: memoryStorage() });
    await expect(w.login('alice.test', 'bad')).rejects.toThrow('Invalid identifier or password');
    expect(w.loggedIn).toBe(false);
    const ok = createAtprotoWriter({ fetchImpl: fakeNetwork(), storage: memoryStorage() });
    await ok.login('alice.test', 'pw');
    await ok.logout();
    expect(ok.loggedIn).toBe(false);
  });
});

===== FILE: frontend/tests/cache.test.js =====
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createCache } from '../src/cache.js';
import { memoryStorage } from './helpers.js';

describe('cache', () => {
  it('round-trips values with savedAt', () => {
    const c = createCache({ storage: memoryStorage(), now: () => 42 });
    c.set('a', { x: 1 });
    expect(c.get('a')).toEqual({ value: { x: 1 }, savedAt: 42 });
    c.remove('a');
    expect(c.get('a')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('never throws when storage throws', () => {
    const bad = { getItem() { throw new Error('denied'); }, setItem() { throw new Error('quota'); }, removeItem() { throw new Error('x'); }, get length() { throw new Error('x'); } };
    const c = createCache({ storage: bad });
    expect(c.set('a', 1)).toBe(false);
    expect(c.get('a')).toBeNull();
    expect(() => c.clear()).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('works with null storage and ignores corrupt entries', () => {
    expect(createCache({ storage: null }).get('a')).toBeNull();
    const s = memoryStorage();
    s.setItem('cm:cache:a', '{not json');
    expect(createCache({ storage: s }).get('a')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('clears only its own prefix', () => {
    const s = memoryStorage();
    s.setItem('other', '1');
    const c = createCache({ storage: s });
    c.set('a', 1);
    c.clear();
    expect(s.getItem('other')).toBe('1');
    expect(c.get('a')).toBeNull();
  });
});

===== FILE: frontend/tests/composeView.test.js =====
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { buildEventRecord, parseTags, validateEventRecord } from '../src/records.js';
import { buildForm, createComposeView, isoToLocalInput, localInputToIso, readForm, showErrors, validateForm } from '../src/views/composeView.js';
import { sampleEvent } from './helpers.js';

const valid = {
  name: 'Study jam', description: '', startsAt: '2026-11-12T18:00', endsAt: '2026-11-12T20:00',
  locationName: 'Geisel', address: '', lat: '', lng: '', tags: '#Study, quiet, study', group: '', hashtag: '',
};

describe('records', () => {
  it('normalizes tags like the backend', () => {
    expect(parseTags(' #Food, music ,food,, ')).toEqual(['food', 'music']);
  });

  it('builds a lexicon-shaped record: strings for lat/lng, omits empties, ISO createdAt', () => {
    const r = buildEventRecord({ name: ' X ', startsAt: 's', endsAt: 'e', locationName: 'L', lat: 32.5, lng: '-117', tags: '', group: '' });
    expect(r).toEqual({ $type: 'app.communitymap.event', name: 'X', startsAt: 's', endsAt: 'e', location: { name: 'L', lat: '32.5', lng: '-117' }, createdAt: r.createdAt });
    expect(new Date(r.createdAt).toISOString()).toBe(r.createdAt);
  });
});

describe('compose validation', () => {
  it('accepts a valid form and converts datetime-local to ISO', () => {
    const { record, errors } = validateForm(valid);
    expect(errors).toEqual({});
    expect(record.startsAt).toBe(new Date('2026-11-12T18:00').toISOString());
    expect(record.tags).toEqual(['study', 'quiet']);
  });

  it('requires name, times and location name', () => {
    const { errors } = validateForm({ ...valid, name: ' ', startsAt: '', endsAt: '', locationName: '' });
    expect(Object.keys(errors).sort()).toEqual(['endsAt', 'locationName', 'name', 'startsAt']);
  });

  it('rejects endsAt before startsAt (equal is allowed)', () => {
    expect(validateForm({ ...valid, endsAt: '2026-11-12T17:59' }).errors.endsAt).toMatch(/after start/);
    expect(validateForm({ ...valid, endsAt: valid.startsAt }).errors).toEqual({});
  });

  it('enforces lexicon lengths and the 10-tag limit', () => {
    const tags = Array.from({ length: 11 }, (_, i) => `t${i}`).join(',');
    const { errors } = validateForm({ ...valid, name: 'x'.repeat(201), description: 'd'.repeat(3001), tags, group: 'g'.repeat(101), address: 'a'.repeat(301) });
    expect(Object.keys(errors).sort()).toEqual(['address', 'description', 'group', 'name', 'tags']);
    expect(validateForm({ ...valid, tags: 'x'.repeat(33) }).errors.tags).toMatch(/32/);
    // byte length, not code units: 67 x 3-byte chars > 200 bytes
    expect(validateEventRecord(buildEventRecord({ ...valid, name: '€'.repeat(67), startsAt: 'x', endsAt: 'x' })).name).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('validates coordinates (both or neither, in range)', () => {
    expect(validateForm({ ...valid, lat: '32.88' }).errors.lat).toMatch(/both/);
    expect(validateForm({ ...valid, lat: '91', lng: '0' }).errors.lat).toMatch(/Latitude/);
    expect(validateForm({ ...valid, lat: 'abc', lng: '0' }).errors.lat).toBeDefined();
    expect(validateForm({ ...valid, lat: '32.88', lng: '-117.23' }).errors).toEqual({});
  });

  it('validates the hashtag', () => {
    expect(validateForm({ ...valid, hashtag: 'two words' }).errors.hashtag).toBeDefined();
    expect(validateForm({ ...valid, hashtag: '#ok' }).errors).toEqual({});
  });

  it('round-trips ISO <-> datetime-local', () => {
    const iso = new Date(2026, 10, 12, 18, 30).toISOString();
    expect(localInputToIso(isoToLocalInput(iso))).toBe(iso);
    expect(localInputToIso('')).toBe('');
  });

  it('marks invalid fields accessibly and focuses the first', () => {
    const form = buildForm(null);
    document.body.replaceChildren(form);
    const { errors } = validateForm(readForm(form));
    showErrors(form, errors);
    expect(form.elements.name.getAttribute('aria-invalid')).toBe('true');
    expect(form.elements.name.getAttribute('aria-describedby')).toBe('c-name-err');
    expect(form.querySelector('#c-name-err').textContent).toMatch(/required/);
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(form.elements.name);
  });

  it('prefills when editing', () => {
    const form = buildForm(sampleEvent());
    expect(readForm(form)).toMatchObject({ name: 'Taco Tuesday', locationName: 'Price Center', lat: '32.8797', tags: 'food, free', group: 'Triton Eats' });
  });
});

describe('compose submit', () => {
  const fill = (form, values) => Object.entries(values).forEach(([k, v]) => { form.elements[k].value = v; });
  const flush = () => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));

  it('creates the event, then a listener for the hashtag', async () => {
    const writer = { createEvent: vi.fn(async () => ({ uri: 'at://d/app.communitymap.event/r1' })), createListener: vi.fn(async () => ({})) };
    const onSaved = vi.fn();
    const view = createComposeView({ getWriter: () => writer, onSaved });
    view.open();
    const form = view.element.querySelector('form');
    fill(form, { ...valid, hashtag: '#StudyJam' });
    form.dispatchEvent(new Event('submit', { cancelable: true }));
    await flush();
    expect(writer.createEvent.mock.calls[0][0]).toMatchObject({ name: 'Study jam', location: { name: 'Geisel' } });
    expect(writer.createListener).toHaveBeenCalledWith('at://d/app.communitymap.event/r1', '#StudyJam');
    expect(onSaved).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('edits with putRecord rkey and preserves createdAt', async () => {
    const writer = { updateEvent: vi.fn(async () => ({ uri: 'u' })), createListener: vi.fn() };
    const view = createComposeView({ getWriter: () => writer });
    const ev = sampleEvent({ createdAt: '2026-11-01T00:00:00.000Z' });
    view.open(ev);
    view.element.querySelector('form').dispatchEvent(new Event('submit', { cancelable: true }));
    await flush();
    const [rkey, record] = writer.updateEvent.mock.calls[0];
    expect(rkey).toBe('3kabc');
    expect(record.createdAt).toBe('2026-11-01T00:00:00.000Z');
    expect(writer.createListener).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('does not submit invalid forms', async () => {
    const writer = { createEvent: vi.fn() };
    const view = createComposeView({ getWriter: () => writer });
    view.open();
    view.element.querySelector('form').dispatchEvent(new Event('submit', { cancelable: true }));
    await flush();
    expect(writer.createEvent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('deletes after confirmation', async () => {
    const writer = { deleteEvent: vi.fn(async () => ({})) };
    const onDeleted = vi.fn();
    const view = createComposeView({ getWriter: () => writer, onDeleted, confirmImpl: () => true });
    await view.remove(sampleEvent());
    expect(writer.deleteEvent).toHaveBeenCalledWith('3kabc');
    expect(onDeleted).toHaveBeenCalledWith(sampleEvent().uri);
  });
});

===== FILE: frontend/tests/devWriter.test.js =====
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { buildCommit, createDevWriter, generateTid } from '../src/devWriter.js';
import { memoryStorage } from './helpers.js';

describe('generateTid', () => {
  it('is 13 base32-sortable chars and monotonic', () => {
    const a = generateTid(1731300000000);
    const b = generateTid(1731300000000);
    const c = generateTid(1731300005000);
    for (const t of [a, b, c]) expect(t).toMatch(/^[234567a-z]{13}$/);
    expect(a < b && b < c).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('dev writer', () => {
  it('posts Jetstream-shaped create commits with a persisted fake DID', async () => {
    const api = { postDevRecord: vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true })) };
    const storage = memoryStorage();
    const w = createDevWriter({ api, storage });
    expect(w.did).toMatch(/^did:plc:demo-[a-z2-7]+$/);
    expect(createDevWriter({ api, storage }).did).toBe(w.did);

    const res = await w.createEvent({ name: 'Jam', createdAt: '2026-11-11T00:00:00Z' });
    const c = api.postDevRecord.mock.calls[0][0];
    expect(c).toEqual({
      did: w.did, time_us: expect.any(Number), kind: 'commit',
      commit: { operation: 'create', collection: 'app.communitymap.event', rkey: expect.stringMatching(/^[234567a-z]{13}$/),
        cid: expect.stringMatching(/^bafy/), record: { $type: 'app.communitymap.event', name: 'Jam', createdAt: '2026-11-11T00:00:00Z' } },
    });
    expect(res.uri).toBe(`at://${w.did}/app.communitymap.event/${c.commit.rkey}`);
  });

  it('update keeps rkey; delete has no record/cid; listener uses listener collection', async () => {
    const api = { postDevRecord: vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true })) };
    const w = createDevWriter({ api, storage: memoryStorage() });
    await w.updateEvent('3kabc', { name: 'n' });
    await w.deleteEvent('3kabc');
    await w.createListener('at://x/app.communitymap.event/3kabc', 'Tag');
    const [u, d, l] = api.postDevRecord.mock.calls.map((x) => x[0].commit);
    expect(u).toMatchObject({ operation: 'update', rkey: '3kabc', record: { name: 'n' } });
    expect(d).toEqual({ operation: 'delete', collection: 'app.communitymap.event', rkey: '3kabc' });
    expect(l).toMatchObject({ operation: 'create', collection: 'app.communitymap.listener', record: { subject: 'at://x/app.communitymap.event/3kabc', hashtag: 'tag' } });
  });

  it('buildCommit uses microsecond time', () => {
    expect(buildCommit({ did: 'd', operation: 'delete', collection: 'c', rkey: 'r', nowMs: 1731300000000 }).time_us).toBe(1731300000000000);
  });
});
