import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { h, safeHttpUrl } from '../src/dom.js';

describe('h()', () => {
  it('escapes text children and sets attributes/handlers', () => {
    const fn = vi.fn();
    const el = h('button', { class: 'b', 'aria-pressed': 'true', onclick: fn, dataset: { k: 'v' } }, '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>', null, ['a', 1]);
    expect(el.querySelector('img')).toBeNull();
    expect(el.textContent).toBe('<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>a1');
    expect(el.className).toBe('b');
    expect(el.dataset.k).toBe('v');
    el.click();
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('safeHttpUrl allows only http(s)', () => {
    expect(safeHttpUrl('https://ucsd.edu/x')).toBe('https://ucsd.edu/x');
    expect(safeHttpUrl('javascript:alert(1)')).toBeNull();
    expect(safeHttpUrl(null)).toBeNull();
  });
});

===== FILE: frontend/tests/filters.test.js =====
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { toQueryString } from '../src/api.js';
import {
  EMPTY_FILTERS, buildEventQuery, coordsOf, groupByDay, haversineKm, isUpdated, sortByDistance, toggleTag,
} from '../src/filters.js';

describe('buildEventQuery', () => {
  it('drops empty values and never sends nearMe', () => {
    expect(buildEventQuery({ ...EMPTY_FILTERS, nearMe: true })).toEqual({ limit: 20 });
  });

  it('includes tags (repeatable), group, trimmed q and cursor', () => {
    const p = buildEventQuery({ tags: ['food', 'music'], group: 'ACM', q: '  tacos ', nearMe: false }, { cursor: 'c1', limit: 5 });
    expect(p).toEqual({ limit: 5, tag: ['food', 'music'], group: 'ACM', q: 'tacos', cursor: 'c1' });
    expect(toQueryString(p)).toBe('?limit=5&tag=food&tag=music&group=ACM&q=tacos&cursor=c1');
  });

  it('toggleTag adds and removes', () => {
    expect(toggleTag(['a'], 'b')).toEqual(['a', 'b']);
    expect(toggleTag(['a', 'b'], 'a')).toEqual(['b']);
  });
});

describe('distance', () => {
  it('haversine is ~0 for same point and right order of magnitude', () => {
    const geisel = { lat: 32.8811, lng: -117.2376 };
    expect(haversineKm(geisel, geisel)).toBe(0);
    // UCSD -> downtown San Diego is ~ 18 km
    const d = haversineKm(geisel, { lat: 32.7157, lng: -117.1611 });
    expect(d).toBeGreaterThan(17);
    expect(d).toBeLessThan(21);
  });

  it('coordsOf parses strings and rejects junk', () => {
    expect(coordsOf({ location: { lat: '32.1', lng: '-117' } })).toEqual({ lat: 32.1, lng: -117 });
    expect(coordsOf({ location: { lat: 'x', lng: '1' } })).toBeNull();
    expect(coordsOf({ location: { lat: '95', lng: '1' } })).toBeNull();
    expect(coordsOf({ location: { name: 'n' } })).toBeNull();
  });

  it('sorts nearest first; no-coords last, stable, annotated', () => {
    const me = { lat: 32.88, lng: -117.23 };
    const far = { uri: 'far', location: { lat: '32.70', lng: '-117.16' } };
    const near = { uri: 'near', location: { lat: '32.881', lng: '-117.231' } };
    const none1 = { uri: 'none1', location: { name: 'x' } };
    const none2 = { uri: 'none2', location: { name: 'y' } };
    const out = sortByDistance([none1, far, none2, near], me);
    expect(out.map((e) => e.uri)).toEqual(['near', 'far', 'none1', 'none2']);
    expect(out[0].distanceKm).toBeLessThan(1);
    expect(out[2].distanceKm).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('groupByDay', () => {
  const now = new Date(2026, 10, 11, 12, 0); // Wed Nov 11 2026 local
  const at = (d, h) => ({ startsAt: new Date(2026, 10, d, h).toISOString(), uri: `${d}-${h}` });

  it('labels Today, Tomorrow, weekday, then date; chronological groups', () => {
    const groups = groupByDay([at(20, 9), at(13, 9), at(12, 9), at(11, 18)], { now, locale: 'en-US' });
    expect(groups.map((g) => g.label)).toEqual(['Today', 'Tomorrow', 'Friday', 'Fri, Nov 20']);
  });

  it('puts in-progress events (started earlier) under Today, keeps order within a group', () => {
    const groups = groupByDay([at(11, 15), at(10, 20), at(11, 13)], { now, locale: 'en-US' });
    expect(groups).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(groups[0].label).toBe('Today');
    expect(groups[0].events.map((e) => e.uri)).toEqual(['11-15', '10-20', '11-13']);
  });
});

describe('isUpdated', () => {
  it('is true only when updatedAt > indexedAt', () => {
    expect(isUpdated({ indexedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z', updatedAt: '2026-01-02T00:00:00Z' })).toBe(true);
    expect(isUpdated({ indexedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z', updatedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z' })).toBe(false);
    expect(isUpdated({})).toBe(false);
  });
});

===== FILE: frontend/tests/helpers.js =====
/** Test helpers. */

/** In-memory Storage implementation. */
export function memoryStorage() {
  const m = new Map();
  return {
    get length() { return m.size; },
    key: (i) => [...m.keys()][i] ?? null,
    getItem: (k) => (m.has(k) ? m.get(k) : null),
    setItem: (k, v) => m.set(k, String(v)),
    removeItem: (k) => m.delete(k),
    clear: () => m.clear(),
  };
}

/** Minimal fetch Response stand-in. */
export function jsonResponse(body, status = 200, headers = {}) {
  return {
    ok: status >= 200 && status < 300,
    status,
    headers: { get: (k) => headers[k.toLowerCase()] ?? null },
    text: async () => (body === undefined ? '' : JSON.stringify(body)),
  };
}

/** Sample EventView. */
export function sampleEvent(over = {}) {
  return {
    uri: 'at://did:plc:abc/app.communitymap.event/3kabc',
    cid: 'bafy1', authorDid: 'did:plc:abc', authorHandle: 'alice.test', source: 'atproto',
    name: 'Taco Tuesday', description: 'Free tacos', startsAt: '2026-11-11T19:00:00Z', endsAt: '2026-11-11T21:00:00Z',
    location: { name: 'Price Center', address: '9500 Gilman Dr', lat: '32.8797', lng: '-117.2362' },
    tags: ['food', 'free'], group: 'Triton Eats', sourceUrl: null,
    indexedAt: '2026-11-10T00:00:00Z', updatedAt: '2026-11-10T00:00:00Z', status: 'upcoming',
    ...over,
  };
}

===== FILE: frontend/tests/listView.test.js =====
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createListView, renderCard } from '../src/views/listView.js';
import { sampleEvent } from './helpers.js';

describe('renderCard', () => {
  it('renders name, time, OSM pin link, group, tags, subscribe toggle', () => {
    const card = renderCard(sampleEvent(), { timeZone: 'America/Los_Angeles', locale: 'en-US' });
    expect(card.querySelector('h3').textContent).toBe('Taco Tuesday');
    expect(card.querySelector('time').getAttribute('datetime')).toBe('2026-11-11T19:00:00Z');
    expect(card.querySelector('time').textContent).toBe('11:00 AM – 1:00 PM');
    const a = card.querySelector('.card__loc a');
    expect(a.href).toContain('openstreetmap.org/?mlat=32.8797&mlon=-117.2362');
    expect(a.rel).toContain('noopener');
    expect(card.textContent).toContain('Triton Eats');
    expect([...card.querySelectorAll('.chip')].map((c) => c.textContent)).toEqual(['#food', '#free']);
    const sub = card.querySelector('[aria-pressed]:not(.chip)');
    expect(sub.textContent).toBe('Subscribe');
  });

  it('falls back to address search when no coordinates', () => {
    const card = renderCard(sampleEvent({ location: { name: 'Geisel', address: '9500 Gilman Dr' } }));
    expect(card.querySelector('.card__loc a').href).toBe('https://www.openstreetmap.org/search?query=9500%20Gilman%20Dr');
  });

  it('shows status badge, updated marker and subscribed state', () => {
    const ev = sampleEvent({ status: 'ending_soon', updatedAt: '2026-11-10T05:00:00Z' });
    const card = renderCard(ev, { subscribed: new Set([ev.uri]) });
    const badges = [...card.querySelectorAll('.badge')].map((b) => b.textContent);
    expect(badges).toEqual(['Ending soon', 'Updated']);
    expect(card.querySelector('.btn').getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    expect(renderCard(sampleEvent({ status: 'live' })).querySelector('.badge').textContent).toBe('Live');
  });

  it('escapes user content', () => {
    const card = renderCard(sampleEvent({ name: '<script>x</script>', group: '<b>g</b>' }));
    expect(card.querySelector('script')).toBeNull();
    expect(card.querySelector('b')).toBeNull();
    expect(card.querySelector('h3').textContent).toBe('<script>x</script>');
  });

  it('fires tag, open and subscribe callbacks', () => {
    const onTagClick = vi.fn(); const onOpen = vi.fn(); const onToggleSubscribe = vi.fn();
    const ev = sampleEvent();
    const card = renderCard(ev, { onTagClick, onOpen, onToggleSubscribe, activeTags: ['food'] });
    const chip = card.querySelector('.chip');
    expect(chip.getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    chip.click();
    card.querySelector('h3 button').click();
    card.querySelector('.card__actions button').click();
    expect(onTagClick).toHaveBeenCalledWith('food');
    expect(onOpen).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ev.uri);
    expect(onToggleSubscribe).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ev.uri);
  });
});

describe('createListView', () => {
  let root;
  beforeEach(() => {
    document.body.innerHTML = '<div id="r"></div>';
    root = document.getElementById('r');
  });
  const now = new Date(2026, 10, 11, 8);
  const ev = (i, day) => sampleEvent({ uri: `at://d/c/${i}`, name: `E${i}`, startsAt: new Date(2026, 10, day, 10).toISOString(), endsAt: new Date(2026, 10, day, 11).toISOString() });

  it('groups cards by day with headings', () => {
    createListView(root, { IntersectionObserverImpl: null }).render({ events: [ev(1, 11), ev(2, 12), ev(3, 12)], now });
    expect([...root.querySelectorAll('h2')].map((x) => x.textContent)).toEqual(['Today', 'Tomorrow']);
    expect(root.querySelectorAll('section')[1].querySelectorAll('article')).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('shows the empty state', () => {
    createListView(root, { IntersectionObserverImpl: null }).render({ events: [], now });
    expect(root.querySelector('.empty')).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('shows the stale/offline banner and errors', () => {
    const v = createListView(root, { IntersectionObserverImpl: null });
    v.render({ events: [ev(1, 11)], stale: true, savedAt: Date.now(), now });
    expect(root.querySelector('.notice--stale').textContent).toMatch(/offline/);
    v.render({ events: [], error: 'Boom', now });
    expect(root.querySelector('[role=alert]').textContent).toBe('Boom');
    expect(root.querySelector('.empty')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('Load more button and IntersectionObserver call onLoadMore (not while loading)', () => {
    let cb;
    const observe = vi.fn();
    class IO { constructor(fn) { cb = fn; } observe(el) { observe(el); } disconnect() {} }
    const onLoadMore = vi.fn();
    const v = createListView(root, { onLoadMore, IntersectionObserverImpl: IO });
    v.render({ events: [ev(1, 11)], hasMore: true, now });
    const btn = root.querySelector('.load-more');
    expect(observe).toHaveBeenCalledWith(btn);
    btn.click();
    cb([{ isIntersecting: true }]);
    expect(onLoadMore).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    v.render({ events: [ev(1, 11)], hasMore: true, loading: true, now });
    cb([{ isIntersecting: true }]);
    expect(onLoadMore).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(root.querySelector('.load-more').disabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it('restores focus to the same control after re-render', () => {
    const v = createListView(root, { IntersectionObserverImpl: null });
    const e = ev(1, 11);
    v.render({ events: [e], now });
    root.querySelector('.card__actions button').focus();
    v.render({ events: [e], subscribed: new Set([e.uri]), now });
    expect(document.activeElement.textContent).toBe('Subscribed');
  });
});

===== FILE: frontend/tests/store.test.js =====
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createStore } from '../src/store.js';

describe('store', () => {
  it('merges patches and notifies with prev state', () => {
    const s = createStore({ a: 1, b: 2 });
    const fn = vi.fn();
    s.subscribe(fn);
    s.set({ a: 3 });
    expect(s.get()).toEqual({ a: 3, b: 2 });
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ a: 3, b: 2 }, { a: 1, b: 2 });
  });

  it('supports functional updates and unsubscribe', () => {
    const s = createStore({ n: 1 });
    const fn = vi.fn();
    const off = s.subscribe(fn);
    s.set((st) => ({ n: st.n + 1 }));
    off();
    s.set({ n: 10 });
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(s.get().n).toBe(10);
  });

  it('does not mutate the initial object', () => {
    const init = { n: 1 };
    createStore(init).set({ n: 2 });
    expect(init.n).toBe(1);
  });
});

===== FILE: frontend/tests/subscriptions.test.js =====
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { backoffMs, createSubscriptions, getSubscriberId, mergeNotifications } from '../src/subscriptions.js';
import { memoryStorage } from './helpers.js';

const n = (id, message = `m${id}`) => ({ id, subscriberId: 's', eventUri: 'at://e', kind: 'announcement', message, createdAt: '2026-11-11T00:00:00Z' });

class FakeES {
  static instances = [];
  constructor(url) { this.url = url; this.closed = false; FakeES.instances.push(this); }
  close() { this.closed = true; }
}

function setup({ storage = memoryStorage(), notifications = [] } = {}) {
  FakeES.instances = [];
  const api = {
    listSubscriptions: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: ['at://e'], stale: false })),
    subscribe: vi.fn(async () => null),
    unsubscribe: vi.fn(async () => null),
    notifications: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: notifications, stale: false })),
    notificationStreamUrl: (id) => `/api/notifications/stream?subscriberId=${id}`,
  };
  const toast = vi.fn();
  const NotificationImpl = Object.assign(vi.fn(), { permission: 'default', requestPermission: vi.fn(async () => 'granted') });
  const subs = createSubscriptions({ api, storage, EventSourceImpl: FakeES, NotificationImpl, toast, baseDelayMs: 10 });
  return { api, toast, subs, storage, NotificationImpl };
}

const flush = () => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));

describe('mergeNotifications', () => {
  it('keeps only newer, deduped, sorted; advances lastId', () => {
    expect(mergeNotifications(5, [n(7), n(4), n(6), n(7), n(5)])).toEqual({ fresh: [n(6), n(7)], lastId: 7 });
    expect(mergeNotifications(5, [])).toEqual({ fresh: [], lastId: 5 });
    expect(mergeNotifications(null, [n(2), n(1)]).lastId).toBe(2);
  });

  it('backoff doubles and caps', () => {
    expect([0, 1, 2, 10].map((i) => backoffMs(i, 1000, 30000))).toEqual([1000, 2000, 4000, 30000]);
  });
});

describe('subscriptions', () => {
  it('persists an anonymous subscriberId', () => {
    const s = memoryStorage();
    const id = getSubscriberId(s);
    expect(id).toBeTruthy();
    expect(getSubscriberId(s)).toBe(id);
  });

  it('subscribe/unsubscribe call the API; permission is requested only once', async () => {
    const { api, subs, NotificationImpl } = setup();
    await subs.toggle('at://a');
    expect(api.subscribe).toHaveBeenCalledWith(subs.subscriberId, 'at://a');
    expect(subs.has('at://a')).toBe(true);
    await subs.toggle('at://a');
    expect(api.unsubscribe).toHaveBeenCalledWith(subs.subscriberId, 'at://a');
    expect(subs.has('at://a')).toBe(false);
    await subs.toggle('at://b');
    expect(NotificationImpl.requestPermission).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('opens the stream only while subscribed and toasts messages', async () => {
    const { subs, toast } = setup();
    await subs.load();
    expect(FakeES.instances).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(FakeES.instances[0].url).toContain(`subscriberId=${subs.subscriberId}`);
    FakeES.instances[0].onmessage({ data: JSON.stringify(n(1, 'Room changed')) });
    expect(toast).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Room changed');
    await subs.toggle('at://e');
    expect(FakeES.instances[0].closed).toBe(true);
  });

  it('first connect records a baseline silently', async () => {
    const { subs, toast, api, storage } = setup({ notifications: [n(3), n(4)] });
    await subs.load();
    FakeES.instances[0].onopen();
    await flush();
    expect(api.notifications).toHaveBeenCalledWith(subs.subscriberId, null);
    expect(toast).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(storage.getItem('cm:last-notification-id')).toBe('4');
  });

  it('on reconnect, catches up via since=<lastId> without duplicating stream messages', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const { subs, toast, api } = setup();
    await subs.load();
    const es1 = FakeES.instances[0];
    es1.onmessage({ data: JSON.stringify(n(10)) });
    es1.onerror();
    expect(es1.closed).toBe(true);
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10);
    const es2 = FakeES.instances[1];
    expect(es2).toBeDefined();
    api.notifications.mockResolvedValueOnce({ data: [n(10), n(11), n(12)], stale: false });
    es2.onopen();
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
    expect(api.notifications).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(subs.subscriberId, 10);
    expect(toast.mock.calls.map((c) => c[0])).toEqual(['m10', 'm11', 'm12']);
    es2.onmessage({ data: JSON.stringify(n(12)) });
    expect(toast).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });
});

===== FILE: frontend/tests/views.test.js =====
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createDetailView, renderDetail } from '../src/views/detailView.js';
import { createFilterBar } from '../src/views/filterBar.js';
import { MAP_CONFIG, buildMarkers, createMapView } from '../src/views/mapView.js';
import { createGeo } from '../src/geo.js';
import { sampleEvent } from './helpers.js';

describe('mapView.buildMarkers', () => {
  it('keeps only events with valid coordinates', () => {
    const ms = buildMarkers([sampleEvent(), sampleEvent({ uri: 'x', location: { name: 'No pin' } })], { timeZone: 'UTC', locale: 'en-US' });
    expect(ms).toEqual([{ uri: sampleEvent().uri, lat: 32.8797, lng: -117.2362, title: 'Taco Tuesday', subtitle: '7:00 PM – 9:00 PM · Price Center' }]);
  });

  it('tile provider is configurable', async () => {
    const L = fakeLeaflet();
    globalThis.L = L;
    const root = document.createElement('div');
    const view = createMapView(root, { config: { tileUrl: 'https://tiles.example/{z}/{x}/{y}.pbf' }, load: async () => L });
    await view.show([sampleEvent()]);
    expect(L.tileLayer).toHaveBeenCalledWith('https://tiles.example/{z}/{x}/{y}.pbf', expect.objectContaining({ maxZoom: MAP_CONFIG.maxZoom }));
    expect(L.marker).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    delete globalThis.L;
  });

  it('shows an error when Leaflet fails to load', async () => {
    const root = document.createElement('div');
    await createMapView(root, { load: async () => { throw new Error('x'); } }).show([]);
    expect(root.querySelector('[role=alert]')).not.toBeNull();
  });
});

function fakeLeaflet() {
  const chain = () => ({ addTo() { return this; }, bindPopup() { return this; }, setView() { return this; }, clearLayers() {}, invalidateSize() {}, fitBounds() {} });
  return { map: vi.fn(chain), tileLayer: vi.fn(chain), layerGroup: vi.fn(chain), marker: vi.fn(chain) };
}

describe('detailView', () => {
  it('renders description and updates newest first, escaped', () => {
    const ev = sampleEvent({ description: '<b>Bring</b> a plate', updates: [
      { text: 'Moved inside', sourceUri: null, createdAt: '2026-11-11T18:00:00Z' },
      { text: 'Starting soon!', sourceUri: null, createdAt: '2026-11-11T18:30:00Z' },
    ] });
    const root = document.createElement('div');
    root.append(...renderDetail(ev).filter(Boolean));
    expect(root.querySelector('.detail__desc').textContent).toBe('<b>Bring</b> a plate');
    expect(root.querySelector('b')).toBeNull();
    expect([...root.querySelectorAll('.updates p')].map((p) => p.textContent)).toEqual(['Starting soon!', 'Moved inside']);
    expect(root.textContent).not.toContain('Edit');
  });

  it('only links http(s) source URLs and shows edit controls for owners', () => {
    const root = document.createElement('div');
    root.append(...renderDetail(sampleEvent({ sourceUrl: 'javascript:alert(1)' }), { canEdit: true }).filter(Boolean));
    expect(root.querySelector('a[href^="javascript"]')).toBeNull();
    expect(root.textContent).toContain('Edit');
  });

  it('loads via api.getEvent and handles 404', async () => {
    const api = { getEvent: vi.fn(async () => { throw Object.assign(new Error('nf'), { status: 404 }); }) };
    const v = createDetailView({ api });
    await v.open('at://x');
    expect(api.getEvent).toHaveBeenCalledWith('at://x');
    expect(v.element.textContent).toContain('no longer available');
  });
});

describe('filterBar', () => {
  it('renders tags/groups and emits changes; search is debounced', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const onChange = vi.fn();
    const root = document.createElement('div');
    document.body.replaceChildren(root);
    const bar = createFilterBar(root, { onChange, debounceMs: 300 });
    bar.render({ tags: ['food'], group: '', q: '', nearMe: false }, { tags: [{ value: 'food', count: 2 }, { value: 'music', count: 1 }], groups: [{ value: 'ACM', count: 4 }] });
    const chips = root.querySelectorAll('.chip');
    expect(chips[0].getAttribute('aria-pressed')).toBe('true');
    chips[1].click();
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ tags: ['food', 'music'] });
    const select = root.querySelector('select');
    expect([...select.options].map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual(['All groups', 'ACM (4)']);
    select.value = 'ACM';
    select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ group: 'ACM' });
    const input = root.querySelector('input[type=search]');
    input.value = 'ta'; input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
    input.value = 'tacos'; input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    vi.advanceTimersByTime(300);
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ q: 'tacos' });
    root.querySelector('button.btn').click();
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith({ nearMe: true });
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });
});

describe('geo', () => {
  it('keeps the position in memory only and can clear it', async () => {
    const geolocation = { getCurrentPosition: (ok) => ok({ coords: { latitude: 32.88, longitude: -117.23 } }) };
    const g = createGeo({ geolocation });
    expect(g.position).toBeNull();
    expect(await g.request()).toEqual({ lat: 32.88, lng: -117.23 });
    expect(g.position).toEqual({ lat: 32.88, lng: -117.23 });
    g.clear();
    expect(g.position).toBeNull();
    await expect(createGeo({ geolocation: null }).request()).rejects.toThrow();
  });
});

It replaces the split-screen layout (#14) and the list/map sync (#16).\n\n# User story\nAs a student browsing campus events, I want a bright, easy-to-read map where I can search, pick a day, and click between events and pins without anything blocking my view, so that I can quickly find what's happening and where.\n\n# Requirements for this task\n\n**Lighter theme**\n- Light theme is the default. Font size at least 16px.\n- Must not cover the event panel or the map controls.\n\n**Map-first layout**\n- The map fills the screen, and the event panel docks on the right (~380–420px wide), with the map visible beside it. The panel can be collapsed to show the full map.\n- The panel switches between a **list view** (event cards, date selector, filters) and a **detail view** (full event info, plus a **← Back to list** button that keeps the scroll position).\n- Mobile: full-screen map, and the panel becomes a bottom sheet the user can drag between peek, half, and full heights.\n- List and detail views have their own URLs (`/`, `/event/:id`), so the browser Back button and shared links work.\n\n**Map ↔ event selection**\n- Clicking an event card opens its details in the panel, moves the map to its pin, and highlights the pin. The pin is never hidden behind the panel.\n- Clicking a pin opens that event's details in the panel. Pin popups are removed so nothing blocks the map. Clicking a cluster zooms in.\n- Hovering a card highlights its pin, and hovering a pin highlights its card. Pins can be reached with the keyboard.\n\n**Date selection**\n- Quick picks at the top of the list: **Today**, **Tomorrow**, **This weekend**, **This week**, **All upcoming** (default).\n- A strip of the next 14 days, with a dot on days that have events, plus a calendar for picking a specific date or a date range.\n- The selected date filters both the list and the map, and combines with the tag/group filters.\n- In **All upcoming**, events are grouped under sticky day headers (\"Today\", \"Fri, Nov 27\").\n- The feed API accepts `from` and `to` parameters. Click **Tomorrow** → only tomorrow's events appear in the list and on the map → tap next Friday in the day strip → only Friday's events show → reload → Friday is still selected.\n6. Select **All upcoming** → events are grouped under day headers that stay pinned while scrolling.\n7. Collapse the panel → the map is full width → reopen the panel.\n8.     "title": "Define the `event` lexicon schema",
    "body": "# Description\nDefine the AT Protocol lexicon for event records. Every other feature (posting, feed, map, tags, groups, subscriptions) reads or writes this schema. The PRD flags breaking schema changes as a key risk, so the schema has to be designed with lexicon evolution rules in mind from day one.\n\n# User story\nAs an event host, I want my event saved in a standard, well-defined format on my own PDS, so that the event is portable and other AT Protocol apps can read it. (PRD 4.4.1 – Interoperability; Host Henry)\n\n# Requirements for this task\n- Evaluate reusing or extending an existing community lexicon (e.g. `community.lexicon.calendar.event`) before writing our own; document the decision as an ADR.\n- If we define our own: NSID `<nsid-root>.event`, where `<nsid-root>` is a domain the team controls (placeholder: `app.communitymap`).\n- Required fields: `name` (string, max length), `startsAt` (datetime), `endsAt` (datetime), `location`, `createdAt` (datetime).\n- Optional fields: `description` (string, max length), `tags` (array of strings, max count and max length each), `group` (strong ref to a group record, see #18).\n- `location` object: `lat`, `lng` (stored as strings or fixed-precision integers, because lexicon has no float type), optional `name` and `address`.\n- Follow [lexicon evolution rules](https://atproto.com/specs/lexicon#lexicon-evolution): new fields are optional, no field is removed or renamed after release.\n- Shared validation module (TypeScript types plus runtime validator) generated from the lexicon and used by both frontend and backend.\n- Unit tests for valid and invalid records (missing fields, `endsAt` before `startsAt`, tag over limit).\n\n# Required tests (e2e using Playwright)\nNo direct UI for this issue. Its E2E coverage comes through #6 and #8. Acceptance for this issue is the unit test suite above passing in CI, plus:\n1. A seeded event record in the local PDS validates against the lexicon.\n2. A malformed record is rejected by the validator with a clear error message.\n",
    "state": "OPEN",

===== FILE: lexicons/app.communitymap.event.json =====
{
  "lexicon": 1,
  "id": "app.communitymap.event",
  "description": "A time-bounded community event or point of interest.",
  "defs": {
    "main": {
      "type": "record",
      "key": "tid",
      "record": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["name", "startsAt", "endsAt", "location", "createdAt"],
        "properties": {
          "name": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 200, "minLength": 1 },
          "description": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 3000 },
          "startsAt": { "type": "string", "format": "datetime" },
          "endsAt": { "type": "string", "format": "datetime", "description": "Event is removed from the index after this time (expiry)." },
          "location": { "type": "ref", "ref": "#location" },
          "tags": { "type": "array", "maxLength": 10, "items": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 32 } },
          "group": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 100, "description": "Organizing club / org / business." },
          "sourceUrl": { "type": "string", "format": "uri" },
          "createdAt": { "type": "string", "format": "datetime" }
        }
      }
    },
    "location": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["name"],
      "properties": {
        "name": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 200 },
        "address": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 300 },
        "lat": { "type": "string", "description": "Decimal degrees as string (lexicons have no float type)." },
        "lng": { "type": "string" }
      }
    }
  }
}

  "lexicon": 1,

  "defs": {
    "main": {
      "type": "record",
      "key": "tid",
      "record": {
        "type": "object",
        "required": ["subject", "hashtag", "createdAt"],
        "properties": {
          "subject": { "type": "string", "format": "at-uri", "description": "at:// URI of an app.communitymap.event owned by the same DID." },
          "hashtag": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 64, "description": "Without the leading #, case-insensitive." },
          "createdAt": { "type": "string", "format": "datetime" }
        }
      }
    }
  }
